### [91] [Monophonic Audio Synthesizer Using FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10116)
*Michael Smith,D. G. Perera*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在FPGA上实现用于专业音频应用的数字信号合成器的设计与实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号合成在电子世界中广泛应用于时钟、信号传输、反馈控制等领域，数字合成通过数字逻辑近似正弦波形，能够精确实现特定频率的波形生成，可应用于数字域或转换为模拟域使用。

Method: 通过在FPGA上实现硬件设计，构建用于专业音频应用的数字合成器。

Result: 成功设计并实现了一种常见的专业音频数字合成器硬件方案。

Conclusion: 证明了在FPGA上实现数字合成器用于专业音频应用的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Signal synthesis is used in every aspect of the electronics world, where sinusoidal waveforms are used to perform functions such as clocking, signal transmission, feedback controls, and other applications. Digital synthesis is the method of approximating sinusoidal waveforms using digital logic, where the waveform is approximated to an accurate degree at a specific frequency which can be either implemented digitally or converted into the analog domain for use elsewhere. This project details the creation of a digital synthesizer commonly used for professional audio applications through the implementation of hardware in an FPGA.

</details>


### [92] [CARB: A Characterization-Guided Framework for CNN Inference Cost Prediction and Deployment Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10506)
*Linh Nguyen,Zhixin Pan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出CARB方法，通过大规模GPU工作负载特性分析，实现对CNN推理成本（能耗、延迟、峰值内存）的高精度预测和快速部署筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着CNN模型在资源受限GPU平台上的部署需求增加，准确预部署估算推理成本（能耗、延迟、峰值内存）变得至关重要。现有方法依赖FLOPs、延迟测量或单设备性能分析作为能耗代理，忽略了架构设计与硬件负载之间的非线性交互关系。

Method: 在两个GPU平台（RTX 5090和RTX 3080）上对13,419个CNN配置进行工作负载特性研究，通过GPU遥测技术分析能耗、延迟和内存的缩放行为；基于特性分析发现，开发了级联混合集成模型CARB，联合预测三个目标，并设计了两阶段部署筛选工作流。

Result: 发现能耗和延迟在高计算需求下存在3倍差异，跨GPU可移植性因目标而异（能耗和延迟需要平台特定模型，而内存可在测试平台间良好迁移）；CARB模型达到R²≈0.99的预测精度；两阶段部署筛选工作流可在数秒内消除超过90%的候选配置，将大型设计空间缩减为经真实硬件验证的帕累托优先短名单。

Conclusion: CNN推理成本的不同维度（能耗、延迟、内存）呈现根本不同的缩放行为和跨平台迁移特性，需要针对性的预测模型；所提出的CARB集成预测方法和工作流筛选机制能够高效准确地支持CNN模型在GPU平台上的部署决策。

Abstract: Accurate pre-deployment estimation of CNN inference cost--energy, latency, and peak memory--is increasingly critical as models are deployed on resource-constrained GPU platforms. Existing approaches rely on FLOPs, latency measurements, or single-device profiling as energy proxies, overlooking the non-linear interactions between architectural design and hardware load. We present a workload characterization study of 13 419 CNN configurations on two GPU platforms (RTX 5090 and RTX 3080) under GPU telemetry, revealing that energy, latency, and memory exhibit fundamentally distinct scaling behaviors: energy and latency diverge by 3x under high computational demand, and cross-GPU transferability differs by target--energy and latency require platform-specific models while memory transfers well across the two tested platforms. Building on these characterization findings, we develop CARB, a cascade-blended ensemble that jointly predicts all three targets with R2 ~0.99, and a two-stage deployment screening workflow that eliminates over 90% of candidates in seconds, reducing large design spaces to a Pareto-prioritized shortlist validated against real hardware.

</details>


### [93] [Adaptive Matrix Multiplication for Dynamic Shapes on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10803)
*Yuhang Zhou,Jiang Peng,Qianyu Jiang,Zhibin Wang,Xinghui Tian,Jianwei Zhou,Songxiang Zhu,Jingyi Zhang,Junsong Wang,Chen Tian*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了AdaptCore框架，通过空间分块和指令编排的系统化解耦，解决了Ascend NPU上矩阵乘法因动态张量形状导致的泛化危机，实现了平均1.85倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵乘法面临由高度动态张量形状驱动的"泛化危机"，这一问题在Ascend NUs上尤为严重，因为其显式控制架构和严格物理约束使得现有以GPU为中心的优化方法无效。

Method: AdaptCore系统地将算子优化解耦为空间分块和指令编排：首先将动态形状映射到硬件感知的2D分块分类法中以平衡片上容量限制和多核并行性；进而集成可组合优化库和确定性分析性能模型，通过数学评估硬件状态突变来主动选择并缓存最优实现。

Result: 在80,000个输入形状上实现了平均1.85倍加速，在代表性端到端模型中相比高度优化的原生供应商库(ACLNN)实现了最高1.48倍加速。

Conclusion: AdaptCore成功解决了Ascend NPU上矩阵乘法的泛化危机，通过系统化的优化解耦和主动实现选择机制，实现了通用高性能矩阵乘法，显著优于现有高度优化的原生库。

Abstract: Matrix Multiplication (MatMul) faces a "generalization crisis" driven by highly dynamic tensor shapes. This crisis is particularly acute on Ascend NPUs, where explicitly controlled architectures and strict physical constraints render existing GPU-centric optimizations ineffective. To resolve this, we propose AdaptCore, an adaptive framework for universally high-performance MatMul on Ascend NPUs. AdaptCore systematically decouples operator optimization into spatial tiling and instruction orchestration. It first maps dynamic shapes into a hardware-aware 2D tiling taxonomy to balance on-chip capacity limits and multi-core parallelism. Furthermore, it integrates a composable optimization library with a deterministic analytical performance model. By mathematically evaluating hardware state mutations, AdaptCore proactively selects and caches optimal implementations, enabling O(1) overhead runtime dispatching. Evaluations demonstrate that AdaptCore delivers a remarkable 1.85x mean speedup across 80,000 input shapes, and achieves up to a 1.48x acceleration in representative end-to-end models over the highly-tuned native vendor library (ACLNN).

</details>


### [94] [You Only Charge Once 2.0 : A End-to-End Analog Computing-in-Memory Architecture with Reconfigurable Switched Capacitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11116)
*Zihao Xuan,Yewen Li,Jia Chen,Wei Xuan,Xiao Huo,Fengbin Tu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Charge-CIM利用开关电容电荷重分配作为统一的计算与转换基板，突破了模拟计算存储中的“ADC墙”瓶颈，显著提升了能效与吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代模拟计算存储(ACiM)加速器受限于"ADC墙"问题：模数转换器消耗大量能量和面积，而位切片执行反复调用这些转换器。现有设计虽通过低分辨率读出或时分复用降低成本，但分别损失输出精度或引入串行化开销。

Method: 提出Charge-CIM架构，采用开关电容电荷重分配作为统一的计算和转换基板。同一电容阵列执行输入转换、模拟MAC、加权移位加法和读出量化，减少独立转换器开销和中间ADC调用。差分读出路径在ADC量化期间合并成对的部分和，实现高度紧凑且能效优化的阵列集成方案。

Result: 在DNN基准测试（从CNN到Transformer模型）上评估，Charge-CIM将ADC能耗降低91.7%，相比最先进的电荷域CIM加速器，能效提升2.7倍，吞吐量提升2.0倍。

Conclusion: Charge-CIM通过统一计算与转换基板有效突破了ACiM的ADC瓶颈，为深度神经网络加速提供了一种高能效、高吞吐量的解决方案。

Abstract: Analog Computing-in-Memory (ACiM) accelerates deep neural networks by keeping weights inside memory arrays and executing dot products in the analog domain. However, modern ACiM accelerators are often limited by the "ADC wall": analog-to-digital converters consume a large fraction of energy and area, while bit-sliced execution repeatedly invokes these converters. Existing designs reduce this cost with low-resolution readout or time multiplexing, but they either lose output fidelity or introduce serialization overhead.
  Charge-CIM addresses this bottleneck by using switched-capacitor charge redistribution as a unified computing and conversion substrate. The same capacitor fabric performs input conversion, analog MAC, weighted shift-and-add, and readout quantization, reducing both standalone converter overhead and intermediate ADC invocations. A differential readout path further combines paired partial sums during ADC quantization, providing a highly compact and energy-efficient solution for array integration. With dataflow architecture support, we evaluated Charge-CIM on a suite of DNN benchmarks, from CNNs to Transformer models, and experimental results show that Charge-CIM reduces ADC energy by 91.7% under our evaluation setup and improves energy efficiency by 2.7x and throughput by 2.0x compared to the state-of-the-art charge-domain CIM accelerator.

</details>


### [95] [When and Where Faults Matter: A Study of Transient Errors in CKKS Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11147)
*Vattana Chan,Matías Mazzanti,Karthik Swaminathan,Augusto Vega,Esteban Mocskos,Radha Venkatagiri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究了全同态加密（FHE）计算中的错误，特别关注未优化CKKS方案中的服务器端同态乘法，发现错误的时机和位置对最终输出正确性有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密（HE）是一种隐私保护加密范式，允许直接在加密数据上进行计算而无需解密。然而，在全同态加密（FHE）计算过程中，错误可能会影响最终输出的正确性，因此需要深入研究这些错误的性质及其影响。

Method: 本文聚焦于未优化的CKKS（Cheon-Kim-Kim-Song）方案中的服务器端同态乘法，分析密文分量c₀和c₁中错误的时机和位置对计算正确性的影响。

Result: 研究表明，密文分量c₀和c₁中错误的时机和位置对最终FHE输出的正确性具有显著影响。

Conclusion: 在全同态加密系统中，错误的时机和位置是影响计算正确性的关键因素，这为理解FHE系统的错误行为和提高其可靠性提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Homomorphic Encryption (HE) is a privacy-preserving encryption paradigm that enables computation directly on encrypted data without requiring decryption. In this paper, we study errors in fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) computations, with a particular focus on server-side homomorphic multiplication in the unoptimized CKKS (Cheon--Kim--Kim--Song) scheme. We show that both the timing and the location of errors in the ciphertext components \(c_0\) and \(c_1\) have a significant impact on the correctness of the final FHE output.

</details>


### [96] [On the Sensitivity to Errors in Homomorphic Computing: Single Transient Bit-flip Client-side Error Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11155)
*Matías Mazzanti,Vattana Chan,Karthik Swaminathan,Augusto Vega,Esteban Mocskos,Radha Venkatagiri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文分析了同态加密（特别是CKKS方案）对比特级故障的敏感性，发现同态乘法是最易受攻击的操作，揭示了HE系统的关键鲁棒性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密（HE）通过噪声注入保障安全，但这种机制引入了内在的错误敏感性。硬件或软件引起的故障可能逃避传统检测机制，导致静默数据损坏，因此需要深入分析HE系统的故障容忍性。

Method: 专注于CKKS方案（广泛用于AI和机器学习中的近似算术），对比特级故障进行敏感性分析，并研究故障在HE管道中的传播和放大机制。

Result: 识别出同态乘法是实际HE管道中对错误最敏感的操作，并刻画了故障通过该操作传播和放大的具体方式。

Conclusion: 暴露了HE系统部署中的关键鲁棒性漏洞，表明需要设计更具弹性的HE部署方案以应对潜在的故障攻击。

Abstract: Homomorphic Encryption (HE) enables computation on encrypted data without decryption and is a key primitive for privacy-preserving computation in sensitive domains such as healthcare, finance, and government. Its security relies on noise injection, which introduces intrinsic error sensitivity and raises concerns about the fault tolerance of HE systems, as hardware- and software-induced faults can evade traditional detection mechanisms and lead to silent data corruption.
  In this work, we analyze the sensitivity of HE to bit-level faults, focusing on the CKKS (Cheon--Kim--Kim--Song) scheme widely used for approximate arithmetic in AI and machine learning workloads. We identify homomorphic multiplication as the most error-sensitive operation in practical HE pipelines and characterize how faults propagate and amplify through it, exposing a critical robustness vulnerability and motivating the need for more resilient HE deployments.

</details>

### [97] [Beyond Cash Flows: A Multi-Agent AI Framework for Valuing Clinical-Stage, Cross-Border Biotechnology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10175)
*Yuhan Fang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专门针对临床阶段生物技术的多智能体投资分析框架，将定性科学判断转化为可防御的估值，并解决跨国市场定价协调和多空观点冲突仲裁问题。该框架基于作者管理中国首个跨境生物技术基金时手动执行的实战方法（16个月回报率127.17%，基准50.67%），为扩展智能体投资系统到复杂、事件驱动型资产类别提供了架构级设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型多智能体投资框架存在关键局限：它们依赖公司可以通过传统现金流进行估值的基础假设。这一范式在临床阶段生物技术领域失效，因为其企业价值完全依赖于二元的科学和监管里程碑，而非传统现金流。当前系统无法有效处理此类复杂、事件驱动型资产类别。

Method: 提出了一种专业化多智能体框架，包含三个核心层次：1）估值层：将定性科学判断转化为预营收资产的可防御估值；2）跨市场协调层：同时协调国际市场的定价；3）冲突融合机制：以领域特定方式系统性地仲裁看涨科学信念与谨慎监管约束之间的冲突。架构设计源自作者作为投资组合经理手工执行并验证的实践方法。

Result: 该框架基于作者管理中国首个专业跨境生物技术基金的实际投资记录：16个月内实现127.17%回报率，超越基准50.67%。这一实绩证明了底层方法论的有效性（注：此处指人工实践方法的有效性，而非AI系统评估结果）。

Conclusion: 本文在架构层面提出了扩展智能体投资系统到复杂、事件驱动型资产类别的 foundational design principles（基础设计原则），填补了当前多智能体框架在临床阶段生物技术等特殊资产类别的空白，为智能体投资分析系统的进一步发展奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: A new class of software systems is transforming investment analysis. Large language model agents assembled into collaborative team structures including analysts, researchers, and risk managers are increasingly deployed across financial markets. Yet current multi-agent frameworks share a critical limitation: they rely on the foundational assumption that companies can be valued through traditional cash flows. This paradigm fails in clinical-stage biotechnology, where enterprise value depends entirely on binary scientific and regulatory milestones. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces a specialized multi-agent framework. Its valuation layer translates qualitative scientific judgment into defensible valuations for pre-revenue assets; its cross-market coordination layer reconciles pricing across international venues simultaneously; and its conflict-fusion mechanism systematically arbitrates between bullish scientific conviction and cautious regulatory constraints in a domain-specific manner. Crucially, the architecture is not a speculative design: it encodes a method the author first executed by hand as sole portfolio manager of China's first dedicated cross-border biotechnology fund, a human practice that returned 127.17% against a 50.67% benchmark within sixteen months. That record is evidence for the underlying method rather than for any AI system; no implementation is evaluated here. This paper presents the framework at the architectural level, establishing foundational design principles for extending agentic investment systems into complex, event-driven asset classes they currently serve poorly.

</details>


### [98] [The Deliberative Deficit: An Empirical Critique of LLMs in Democratic Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10186)
*Maurice Flechtner*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LLMs在多元价值问题上虽有程序性表现良好，但缺乏人类视角多样性和主体间一致性，适合作为辅助工具而非自主审议代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在需要多元价值判断的复杂问题中被广泛部署，但现有可验证任务基准（数学、编程、协调博弈）无法完全评估其在无客观正确答案问题上的推理能力，而程序性评价（尊重性、正当性、参与性）又系统性地不足。

Method: 将政治学领域开发的审议推理指数（DRI）应用于LLM群体推理评估，综合分析了12个公民大会议题、11种前沿模型配置下的1,980次五智能体LLM运行数据，并与人类审议进行比较。

Result: LLM群体的程序性质量与人类审议相当，但在主体间一致性方面增益较小且依赖话题；视角多样性约为人类议会的三分之一，且呈现与人类相反的收敛模式——人类审议减少分歧而LLM审议增加分歧；通过人格提示工程化多样性虽能调整审议推理更新的组件，但未能恢复人类动态。

Conclusion: LLMs可以作为支持人类处理多元问题的工具，但当前证据不支持将其视为自主的审议代理。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in settings that require collective reasoning on complex, value-laden problems. Confidence in these deployments rests largely on benchmarks for verifiable tasks (mathematics, coding, coordination games), yet many of these applications concern problems where no objectively correct answer exists and where decision quality instead depends on integrating pluralistic perspectives to find mutually acceptable solutions. We argue that LLM reasoning capacity on this class of problems cannot be fully inferred from verifiable-task benchmarks, and that procedural evaluations of LLM discourse (respectfulness, justification, engagement) are systematically insufficient. We apply the Deliberative Reason Index (DRI), a measure developed in political science and validated across citizen assemblies, as a tool for evaluating reliable group-level reasoning on pluralistic, non-verifiable problems. Synthesizing recent evidence across 1,980 five-agent LLM runs on 12 citizen-assembly topics across 11 frontier model configurations, we find that LLM groups produce discourse with procedural quality comparable to human deliberation, while gains in intersubjective consistency are small, topic-dependent, and concentrated on tractable rather than ethically contested questions. LLM groups exhibit roughly one-third the perspective diversity of human assemblies and reverse the human convergence pattern: human deliberation decreases dispersion as diverse views synthesise, whereas LLM deliberation increases it. Engineering diversity through persona prompting does not restore the human dynamic but inverts which component of deliberative reasoning is updated. Our conclusion is constraining rather than prohibitive: LLMs can function as tools supporting human reasoning on pluralistic problems, but current evidence does not license treating them as autonomous deliberative agents.

</details>


### [99] [Not a Monolith: Lab-Level Divergence in the Cooperative Equilibria of Chinese Frontier LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10262)
*Francisco León Zúñiga Bolívar*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 通过进化迭代囚徒困境实验，发现四款中国前沿大模型在合作倾向上存在显著差异，"中国模型"不应被视为单一整体，实验室层面才是合作倾向设定的关键单位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究西方前沿大语言模型代理所表现出的合作偏见是否延伸至不同的对齐谱系，以及体现该谱系的中国模型是否应被视为单一整体还是不同实验室的独立实体。

Method: 在进化迭代囚徒困境中研究四款前沿级中国模型（DeepSeek V4 Pro、Qwen3-Max、Kimi K2.5和GLM-5.1），采用固定转换器（GPT-5.4 Mini）的设计消除先前研究中存在的混淆因素，进行全对全锦标赛和Moran过程（每种条件n=500次运行），涵盖三种提示风格和四种种群机制。

Result: 支持H6假设：四家实验室在攻击性均衡比例P_A上存在显著差异，从Qwen3-Max的1%到DeepSeek V4 Pro的9%，六项两两比较中有四项通过Holm-Bonferroni检验；四家实验室间的P_A差异范围（8个百分点）大于中西方生态系统平均P_A差异（均为5.0%），生态系统内部变异超过东西方差距。H5假设得到有条件支持：12项实验室-提示组合中有6项呈现合作多数，低于西方模型报告的12项中的9项，但在预注册稳健性检验中使用替代转换器时上升至9/12。

Conclusion: 实验室而非生态系统是合作倾向设定的单位，将"中国模型"视为单一整体缺乏证据支持，生态系统内部差异可能超过跨生态系统差异。

Abstract: Does the cooperative bias documented for Western frontier LLM agents extend to a different alignment lineage, and should the Chinese models that embody it be treated as a single bloc or as distinct laboratories? We study four frontier-tier Chinese models - DeepSeek V4 Pro, Qwen3-Max, Kimi K2.5 and GLM-5.1 - in an evolutionary Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma, under a design that removes a confound present in prior work. Rather than letting each model convert its own natural-language strategies into code, which entangles strategic disposition with coding ability, we hold the converter fixed (GPT-5.4 Mini) across all labs, so every cross-lab comparison is a comparison of generation alone. We run the full protocol: all-play-all tournaments and a Moran process at n=500 runs per condition, across three prompt styles and four population regimes. Two pre-registered hypotheses are evaluated. H6 (not monolithic) is supported: the four labs differ significantly in aggressive-equilibrium proportion, P_A running from 1% for Qwen3-Max to 9% for DeepSeek V4 Pro, with four of six pairwise comparisons surviving Holm-Bonferroni. The spread across the four labs (P_A range 8pp) is larger than the difference between the Chinese and Western ecosystems' mean P_A (5.0% vs 5.0%): on this measure, within-ecosystem variation exceeds the East-West gap. H5 (cooperative-bias generality) is consistent but qualified: a cooperative plurality holds in 6 of 12 lab-prompt combinations against the 9 of 12 reported for Western models, a difference we do not treat as firm, since the count rests on Cooperative-Neutral near-ties and rises to 9/12 under an alternate converter in our pre-registered robustness check. The lab, not the ecosystem, is the unit at which cooperative disposition is set; treating "Chinese models" as a monolith is not supported by the evidence.

</details>


### [100] [ASCon: A Direction-Aware Reciprocal Agent--Step Contextualization Model for Failure Attribution in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10646)
*Shuyu Jiang,Yue Ran,Kaiyu Xu,Xingshu Chen,Yi Zhang,Hao Ren,Rui Tang,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了ASCon，一个方向感知的智能体-步骤上下文化模型，用于LLM多智能体系统的故障归因，通过统一表示模型同时识别故障智能体、错误步骤和故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM多智能体系统故障归因方法主要针对特定归因目标开发专用模型，忽视了不同归因目标（故障智能体、错误步骤、故障模式）之间的证据依赖关系。这些目标虽然不同，但都依赖于来自MAS轨迹的共同诊断证据，包括任务约束、智能体角色、行为历史和智能体间交互。这种共性促使研究者开发统一表示模型。

Method: 提出ASCon（方向感知的智能体-步骤上下文化模型）：1）引入方向感知图注意力机制建模执行上下文；2）使用掩码步骤到智能体注意力构建行为感知的智能体表示；3）采用智能体条件化的步骤上下文化将智能体上下文融入步骤表示；4）通过轻量级目标特定头部实现不同归因目标。该模型将轨迹证据聚合到单个智能体和步骤表示中。

Result: 实验表明ASCon在故障智能体检测上提升5.83%+微准确率，在错误步骤检测上提升10.63%+微准确率，在故障模式检测上提升14.73%+ Macro-F1分数。同时显著增强了基于LLM方法在域外场景下的归因能力。

Conclusion: ASCon通过统一表示框架成功整合了多智能体系统故障归因的共同证据，证明了考虑不同归因目标间依赖关系的有效性，为LLM多智能体系统的故障诊断提供了更加全面和准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Failure attribution in LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) aims to answer who caused failures, when they occurred, and why by identifying responsible targets including faulty agents, erroneous steps, and failure modes. Existing methods have primarily focused on developing dedicated models for specific attribution targets, with limited attention to the evidential dependencies among them. Despite these attribution targets are different, they rely on common diagnostic evidence from MAS trajectories, including task constraints, agent roles, behavioral histories and inter-agent interactions. This commonality motivates us to develop a unified representation model that aggregates the trajectory evidence into individual agent and step representations, which can subsequently be adapted to different attribution targets. Accordingly, we propose ASCon, a direction-aware reciprocal \textbf{A}gent--\textbf{S}tep \textbf{Con}textualization model for multiple failure attribution targets. ASCon introduces direction-aware graph attention to model execution context, masked step-to-agent attention to construct behavior-aware agent representations, and agent-conditioned step contextualization to incorporate agent context back into step representations. The resulting contextualized representations enable different attribution targets through lightweight target-specific heads. Experiments show that ASCon can improve faulty-agent detection by 5.83\%+ in micro-accuracy, faulty-step detection by 10.63\%+ in micro-accuracy, and failure-mode detection by 14.73\%+ in Macro-F1. Meanwhile, it can also substantially enhance the LLM-based methods' attribution capabilities in out-of-domain scenarios.

</details>


### [101] [Reifying Research Logic: AI-Assisted Workflow Construction and Incremental Refinement for Quantitative Syntax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10662)
*He Wang,Jingbo Chen,Yuqiao Lai,Nan Yang,Hanwen Zhang,Wei Yuan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了QLWF可视化工作流平台，通过AI辅助的五阶段流程将自然语言研究描述转化为可执行工作流，在64任务基准测试中达到98.4%的输出合理性，增量修正仅消耗完全重新生成约三分之一的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量语言研究依赖长链计算步骤，但连接这些步骤的逻辑通常埋藏在脚本中，导致分析难以检查、共享和修改。需要一种方法使研究逻辑可视化并支持可重现执行。

Method: 提出QLWF视觉工作流平台，采用AI辅助的五阶段流程：通过具体化使研究逻辑可视化为工作流，通过形式化赋予工作流确定性执行语义。语言模型仅用于构建阶段，执行由固定节点库和引擎处理以确保可重现性，支持增量修正而非完全重建。

Result: 在QL-Bench 64任务基准测试上，QLWF在三次运行中为每个任务生成结构有效且可执行的工作流，平均输出合理性达98.4%，显著高于基于提示的基线。在12任务生命周期基准测试上，增量修正每次都成功，token消耗约为完全重新生成的三分之一。

Conclusion: QLWF成功实现了定量句法研究工作流的可视化、形式化和可重现执行，通过增量修正提高了效率。发布了节点库、基准测试、工作流模板和平台作为可重用资源，为定量句法研究提供了有价值的工具支持。

Abstract: Quantitative language research often depends on long chains of computational steps, yet the logic connecting those steps usually remains buried in scripts. This makes analyses harder to inspect, share, and revise than they need to be. Focusing on quantitative syntax, we present QLWF, a visual workflow platform that turns natural-language research descriptions into executable workflows through an AI assisted five-stage pipeline. In this setting, reification makes the research logic visible as a workflow, while formalization gives that workflow deterministic execution semantics. The language model is used only during construction. Execution is handled by a fixed node library and engine, which keeps the resulting workflows reproducible. QLWF also supports incremental refinement, so saved workflows can be revised by changing only the parts that need to change rather than being rebuilt from scratch. To evaluate the approach, we build a 64-task benchmark called QL-Bench from the quantitative-syntax literature. Across three runs, QLWF produces structurally valid and executable workflows for every task and reaches a mean output-plausibility rate of 98.4%, well above the prompt-based baselines. On a separate 12-task lifecycle benchmark, this refinement process succeeds in every case and uses roughly one-third of the tokens required by full regeneration. The paper also releases the node library, benchmark, workflow templates, and platform as reusable resources for quantitative-syntax research.

</details>


### [102] [Who Are You Explaining To? A Multi-Agent System for Audience-Aware XAI Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11033)
*Francesco Musicco,Danilo Danese,Giuseppe Fasano,Angela Lombardi,Alberto Carlo Maria Mancino,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了XstrAI，一个面向医疗AI的受众感知多智能体框架，通过将局部解释编码为不可变的结构化表示，并利用三个专门的LLM智能体进行受众感知规划、语言实现和验证，生成针对不同受众（患者、临床医生、数据科学家）的忠实且适当的解释叙述，在糖尿病和卒中风险预测任务中优于11个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的特征归因方法（如SHAP）虽然为模型预测提供了有用的证据，但其数值输出对于具有不同专业知识、目标和误解释风险的受众来说往往不够充分。在医疗AI领域，同一局部解释需要通过截然不同的沟通形式传达给患者、临床医生和数据科学家。直接使用大语言模型进行朴素语言化存在 grounding 弱、将归因与因果语言混淆、以及输出具有说服力但不忠实于底层模型证据等问题。

Method: 提出XstrAI框架，将每个预测案例编码为不可变的结构化表示，在所有受众间共享以确保底层证据固定。生成过程分解为三个专门的LLM智能体：负责受众感知规划、语言实现、以及针对grounding、归因一致性、沟通风险和受众适当性的验证，并在检测到不一致时触发有界的修订循环。

Result: 在糖尿病和卒中风险预测任务上，与从直接语言化到最先进叙述器重实现的11个基线进行对比。评估结合了测量对SHAP证据忠实度的叙述内机制和通过参考语料库、多系列LLM评判者和目标读者调查评估受众适当性的叙述外机制。XstrAI的叙述被独立评判者一致地分配给其目标受众，在临床医生和患者受众上优于所有基线，在数据科学家受众上与表现最佳的单提示基线具有竞争力。

Conclusion: XstrAI通过将局部解释视为固定证据并结构化其沟通方式，成功解决了医疗AI解释中受众多样性和忠实性之间的平衡问题，为不同受众提供了适当且忠实的模型解释叙述，证明了多智能体方法在受众感知解释生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods such as SHAP provide useful evidence about individual model predictions, but their numerical outputs are rarely sufficient for audiences with different expertise, goals, and risks of misinterpretation. In medical AI, the same local explanation must reach patients, clinicians, and data scientists through markedly different forms of communication, and naive verbalization through large language models (LLMs) is prone to weak grounding, conflation of attribution with causal language, and outputs that are persuasive without being faithful to the underlying model evidence. We introduce XstrAI, an audience-aware multi-agent framework that treats local explanations as fixed evidence and structures how it is communicated to each target reader. Each prediction case is encoded as an immutable structured representation, shared identically across audiences so the underlying evidence remains fixed. Generation is factored into three specialized LLM agents responsible for audience-aware planning, linguistic realization, and validation for grounding, attribution consistency, communicative risk, and audience appropriateness, with a bounded revision loop triggered on detected inconsistencies. We evaluate XstrAI on diabetes and stroke risk prediction against 11 baselines, ranging from direct verbalization to a re-implementation of a state-of-the-art narrator. The evaluation combines an intra-narrative regime measuring fidelity to SHAP evidence with an extra-narrative regime assessing audience appropriateness through reference corpora, multi-family LLM judges, and a survey with target readers. In both evaluations, XstrAI's narratives are consistently assigned to their intended audience by independent judges, and preferred over all baselines on Clinician and Patient audiences, with competitive performance on Data Scientist, where audience-conditioned single-prompt baselines lead.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于大语言模型的自主实验框架，通过49通道植物传感器网络实现从数据分析到自动化控制的转变，在垂直农场应用中实现生产周期缩短35%、能耗降低18%以及节能67.9%的显著成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生物系统研究依赖人工分析和专家干预，存在计算资源和专业劳动力限制。随着大语言模型能力提升，迫切需要探索其在复杂生物系统中的自主化应用，建立直接的AI-生物学接口，以降低专家依赖并提升数字农业的成本效益比。

Method: 构建基于大语言模型的闭环控制框架，整合49通道植物传感器网络（包含多光谱、电化学和介电模态）。系统通过自然语言解释提升可访问性，核心是从人在环分析过渡到自主控制。LLM处理生物物理数据评估植物生理状态，触发硬件执行器优化微气候、执行表型分析协议或诱导可控胁迫场景。在垂直农场和生产规模部署中验证，通过生物传感遥测数据调控全光谱、450nm和660nm照明。

Result: 三个案例研究验证了框架有效性。生产规模部署中，智能体执行多参数优化，平衡生物量积累、叶绿素含量和能耗。最小时间模式下生产周期缩短35%；能耗优化模式在培养时间仅边际增加的情况下减少18%能耗，利用光脉冲的生理惯性；智能体自主开发了暗诱导叶绿素积累策略，实现67.9%节能。系统成功解读植物生理学的复杂微观和宏观波动。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将大语言模型转化为数字农业的自主副驾驶，建立了直接的AI-生物学接口，实现了数据驱动的复杂生物系统和生态探索。通过降低计算和专家劳动约束，改善了成本效益比，为AI引导的自主化生物实验提供了可行路径，推动数字农业向智能化、自动化方向发展。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption. The LLM processed biosensing telemetry to modulate full-spectrum, 450 nm, and 660 nm lighting at 2-hour intervals. Compared to periodic control, the system in minimal-time mode reduced the production cycle by 35%. In the energy-optimization mode, it reduced energy consumption by 18% with only a marginal increase in cultivation time, exploiting physiological inertia via light pulses. Finally, the agents autonomously developed an unforeseen strategy of dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation, resulting in a 67.9% energy saving. This framework transforms LLMs into autonomous co-pilots for digital agriculture, improving the cost-to-value ratio and lowering computational and expert-labor constraints.

</details>


### [104] [SPOTting the Future: Lookahead Explanations for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09967)
*Tamar Gozlan,Claudia V. Goldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SPOT框架，通过构建可解释的有限时域树来解释深度强化学习策略的决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习代理在复杂环境中表现优异，但其决策过程难以解释，缺乏有效的解释方法。

Method: 提出SPOT（Sampling Policy Observation Tree）模型无关的采样框架，通过采样动作并递归模拟后续状态来构建可解释的有限时域树，提供策略动作偏好的经验表示。

Result: 提供形式化保证，证明SPOT能够渐近恢复策略的唯一最可能动作，并刻画高熵策略下的分歧行为；在SUMO-RL交通信号控制领域的案例研究中展示了其有效性。

Conclusion: SPOT的树状表示可用于检查策略偏好、比较替代未来轨迹，并揭示单时间步特征归因方法无法观察到的下游行为，为DRL策略解释提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents achieve strong performance in complex environments, yet their decision-making processes remain difficult to interpret. We introduce SPOT (Sampling Policy Observation Tree), a novel model-agnostic, sampling-based framework for interpreting DRL policies. Given access to the policy and an environment simulator, SPOT constructs an interpretable finite-horizon tree by sampling actions and recursively simulating the resulting successor states. The tree provides an empirical representation of the policy's action preferences and their possible downstream evolution. We provide formal guarantees establishing SPOT's asymptotic recovery of the policy's unique most probable action and characterizing its disagreement behavior under high-entropy policies. We demonstrate SPOT in the SUMO-RL traffic-signal control domain. The case study illustrates how its tree-based representation can be used to inspect policy preferences, compare alternative future trajectories, and reveal downstream behaviors that are not visible through single-timestep feature-attribution methods.

</details>


### [105] [MIDAS: Mutual Information Disentanglement with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09986)
*Yuhua Wen,Yingying Zhou,Qifei Li,Yingming Gao,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIDAS框架，通过变分建模和互信息解耦处理不完整多模态数据，引入不确定性感知融合机制，在多个数据集上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析方法假设输入完整，但实际应用中常遇到模态缺失或损坏问题。现有基于数据插补和启发式约束的方法无法有效从不完整数据中提取任务相关信息。

Method: 采用变分建模策略用多元高斯潜变量表示各模态，将其分解为共享和独有因子。设计极小极大目标函数：最小化共享与独有空间的互信息以实现稳定解耦，最大化跨模态共享空间互信息以增强语义对齐。引入不确定性感知融合机制，利用后验方差作为可靠性指标自适应调整融合权重。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上进行了大量实验，MIDAS在各种不完整设置下相比竞争基线获得了强劲且一致的性能提升，证明了其在处理不完整数据场景中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIDAS框架能够有效处理不完整多模态数据的情感分析任务，通过互信息解耦和不确定性感知融合机制，在模态缺失情况下仍能保持良好的性能表现，具有较强的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Most existing multimodal sentiment analysis approaches assume access to complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world applications frequently encounter incomplete or corrupted modalities, posing a critical challenge. Although several methods have been proposed to tackle this issue, they mainly rely on data imputation and heuristic coordination constraints, which fail to effectively extract and leverage task-relevant information from the incomplete multimodal data. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework termed Mutual Information Disentanglement with uncertainty-Aware fuSion (MIDAS), which effectively restructures multimodal representations under incomplete conditions. MIDAS adopts a variational modeling strategy to represent each modality with multivariate Gaussian latent variables and further decomposes them into shared and exclusive factors. To obtain reliable representations, we design a minimax objective that minimizes the mutual information between shared and exclusive spaces for stable disentanglement, while maximizing the mutual information among shared spaces across modalities to enhance semantic alignment. In addition, an uncertainty-aware fusion mechanism is introduced, where posterior variance is leveraged as a reliability indicator to adaptively weight latent features during fusion, ensuring robust integration even when modalities are incomplete. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that MIDAS achieves strong and consistent performance gains over competitive baselines across a wide range of incomplete settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness for incomplete data scenarios.

</details>


### [106] [Towards Sustainable Artificial Intelligence: A Comprehensive Review and Comparative Analysis of Deep Learning Models' Carbon Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09998)
*Samar Garrab,Sarra Boughriou,Manel BenSassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性综述了绿色AI研究，并通过实验发现深度学习模型的训练阶段是碳排放的主要来源，模型复杂度的增加并不总能带来相应的性能提升，强调了在模型选择时平衡性能与环境成本的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI和ML（特别是深度学习）的广泛应用，其高能耗和碳排放问题日益突出，迫切需要研究如何降低AI模型的环境影响，实现可持续发展。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法梳理绿色AI和绿色深度学习的优化技术；比较多种碳测量工具；在CPU环境下实验，实现六种深度学习模型进行多标签分类任务，量化并比较各模型的碳排放，分析生命周期各阶段的排放贡献。

Result: 训练阶段是深度学习模型碳排放的主要来源；模型架构复杂度的增加并不系统性地带来准确率的提升；预测性能与环境成本需仔细权衡。

Conclusion: 在模型选择和AI系统设计中需整合可持续性考量，推动绿色AI的发展，以减少AI技术对环境的负面影响。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) have become powerful tools for supporting and automating complex human tasks. Despite their benefits, growing attention has been directed toward their environmental implications, primarily due to their high energy demands and associated carbon emissions. This concern is particularly relevant in light of the increasing deployment of large-scale models, especially Deep Learning (DL) architectures, which provide advanced predictive capabilities but require substantial computational resources. This paper presents a systematic review of research on Green AI, Green DL, and optimization techniques aimed at reducing the environmental impact of AI models. In addition, we examine and compare several carbon measurement tools for estimating emissions generated by AI algorithms. To complement the review, we conducted an empirical evaluation using a CPU-based experimental setup, in which six DL models were implemented for a multi-label classification task. The objective was to quantify and compare their overall carbon emissions and to determine which stages of the DL lifecycle contribute most significantly to the total footprint. The results show that the training phase is the primary source of emissions. Moreover, the findings reveal that increased architectural complexity does not systematically translate into proportional accuracy gains, highlighting the importance of carefully balancing predictive performance and environmental cost. These results reinforce the need to integrate sustainability considerations into model selection and AI system design.

</details>


### [107] [ReCBM: Uncertainty-Gated Relational Reasoning for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10004)
*An Sui,Yuzhu Li,Fuping Wu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReCBM框架，通过在概念瓶颈模型中引入不确定性门控的关系推理机制，有效解决了不可靠概念状态下的鲁棒推理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）虽然通过人类可理解的概念提供了可解释性，但在面对不可靠概念状态时缺乏鲁棒的推理能力。误导性的语义证据可能通过瓶颈传播，影响解释质量和下游预测准确性。

Method: 提出ReCBM（不确定性门控关系推理框架）：1）在瓶颈层引入语义定义的概念关系；2）利用不确定性引导关系细化；3）通过建模共现、蕴含和排斥关系来规范概念间的证据交换；4）使用不确定性调节每个概念在推理过程中的贡献度。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明：1）在概念缺失和翻转情况下改善了概念恢复和任务恢复能力；2）支持不确定性感知的干预；3）能够提取紧凑的任务相关概念子集；4）在保持下游性能不下降的同时提升了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ReCBM成功地将不确定性引导的关系推理集成到CBMs中，显著提升了模型在不可靠概念状态下的推理鲁棒性，为可解释AI系统提供了更可靠的预测和解释机制。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an interpretable framework by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts, enabling semantic inspection and test-time intervention. Recent variants have improved CBMs through richer concept representations, uncertainty estimation, and dependency modeling. However, robust reasoning under unreliable concept states remains underexplored. Without such reasoning, misleading semantic evidence can propagate through the bottleneck, compromising both explanations and downstream predictions. To address this issue, we propose ReCBM, an uncertainty-gated relational reasoning framework for CBMs. ReCBM introduces semantically defined concept relations into the bottleneck and uses uncertainty to guide their refinement. By modeling co-occurrence, implication, and exclusion, ReCBM specifies how evidence is exchanged across concepts, while uncertainty modulates the contribution of each concept during this process. Experiments across diverse datasets showed that ReCBM improved concept and task recovery under missing and flipped concepts, supported uncertainty-aware intervention, and extracted compact task-relevant concept subsets without degrading downstream performance.

</details>


### [108] [Automating and Scaling Behavioral Scientific Research on AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10030)
*Soo Yong Lee,Jongha Lee,Jaewan Chun,Hyunjin Hwang,Fanchen Bu,Ziv Ben-Zion,Taekwan Kim,Denny Borsboom,Jaemin Yoo,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了AEROBAT，首个用于自动化AI智能体行为科学研究的多人系统，能够自动完成从假设生成到报告撰写的完整研究流程，在12个目标行为上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在复杂环境中的部署日益增加，理解其行为变得至关重要。然而，目前对AI智能体的行为科学研究仍然依赖人工方式，劳动强度大且效率低下，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 引入AEROBAT多人系统，该系统能够针对用户指定的任意目标行为，自动执行完整的行为科学研究流程：生成行为假设、设计和执行对照实验、进行行为评估、分析结果并撰写报告。

Result: 在12个目标行为上，AEROBAT生成并测试了79个假设，设计了1,240个对照实验并执行了总计23,512轮模拟。其中26个假设获得了中等至较强的统计证据支持，包括一些新颖的发现。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，对AI智能体的自动化行为科学研究能够补充和扩展人工研究的覆盖范围，为AI行为分析提供了有效的自动化工具。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly deployed in complex environments, understanding their behaviors becomes critical. Yet behavioral scientific research on AI agents remains manual and labor-intensive. We introduce AEROBAT, the first multi-agent system to automate behavioral scientific research on AI agents. Given an arbitrary target behavior by its user, AEROBAT automatically executes a full pipeline of behavioral scientific research---generating hypotheses about the behavior, designing and executing controlled experiments, making behavioral assessments, analyzing the results, and writing reports. For 12 target behaviors, we used AEROBAT to generate and test 79 hypotheses: designing 1,240 controlled experiments and executing 23,512 simulation rounds in total. Moderate-to-strong statistical evidence was found for 26 hypotheses, including some novel ones. In sum, our results demonstrate that automated behavioral scientific research on AI agents can complement and extend the reach of manual research.

</details>


### [109] [TRIBE: Predicting Team Performance via Communication Behavior Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06926)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali,Nikolos Gurney,David V. Pynadath,Fred Morstatter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TRIBE方法，通过分析团队沟通模式预测团队表现，能将团队分类为行为族群，并在任务进行10%时即可实现早期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计有效辅助人类团队的自主智能体需要理解团队动态，但往往缺乏任务特定知识。传统的绩效指标难以揭示团队行为动态，需要一种领域无关的方法来早期识别团队行为模式。

Method: 提出TRIBE方法，通过分析团队的沟通模式来识别团队行为动态。该方法能够将团队分类为具有预测性的行为族群，并进行了时间分析以评估AI智能体和人类顾问对团队行为轨迹的影响。同时将TRIBE与Llama模型进行比较，并对流程进行优化。

Result: 在四个多样化数据集上验证：1）沟通模式能预测团队表现，预测强度随任务结构允许的行为自由度变化；2）在任务进行10%时即可预测团队表现；3）AI智能体会显著改变团队行为轨迹，而人类顾问与自然动态一致；4）团队在整个协作过程中保持行为灵活性；5）优化后相比Llama实现了显著加速和性能提升。

Conclusion: TRIBE作为一种领域无关的方法，能够有效揭示传统绩效指标无法观察到的团队行为动态，为团队早期干预提供了可能，并为设计辅助人类团队的自主智能体提供了重要洞察。

Abstract: Designing autonomous agents that effectively assist human teams hinges on understanding team dynamics, often without task specific knowledge. We present TRIBE, a domain independent approach that reveals team behavioral dynamics invisible to traditional performance metrics. We show that communication patterns can categorize teams into performance predictive behavioral tribes, as early as 10% into the task, enabling timely interventions. We test TRIBE on four diverse datasets and demonstrate that communication patterns predict team performance while the prediction strength varies by the degree a task structure allows for behavioral freedom. Our temporal analysis reveals that AI agents significantly alter team behavioral trajectories while human advisors align with natural dynamics, and that teams maintain behavioral flexibility throughout collaboration. Further, we compare TRIBE to Llama and optimize the pipeline, achieving significant speedup with performance improvement.

</details>


### [110] [CHORUS: Complementary Experts for High-Coverage Testbench Stimulus Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10090)
*Hejia Zhang,Sheng Lu,Zhongming Yu,Chia-Tung Ho,Brucek Khailany,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHORUS是一个后训练框架，通过结合分阶段SFT和密集奖励RL产生的多样化专家模型，在硬件验证测试平台生成任务上实现了超越传统SFT-to-RL流程的性能，4B参数模型在CVDP-ECov上达到88.0% Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码生成领域取得进展，可执行反馈比纯文本模仿提供更可靠的学习信号。硬件验证是代码生成的重要应用场景，占现代芯片设计工作的重要部分，其中高覆盖率测试平台激励生成是关键任务。传统监督微调(SFT)到强化学习(RL)的管道存在性能瓶颈。

Method: CHORUS框架基于两个核心观察：(1)分阶段SFT产生行为多样化的检查点，密集奖励RL将其转化为具有相当总体性能但不同任务级优势的强专家模型；(2)通过无训练模型合并或进一步后训练可以整合这些互补优势，超越最佳单专家模型性能。

Result: 通过将生成的专家模型整合为单个4B参数模型，CHORUS在CVDP-ECov基准上达到88.0% Pass@1准确率，比DeepSeek-R1(671B参数)高出13.5个百分点。

Conclusion: CHORUS证明了利用多样化专家模型的互补优势可以有效突破传统训练管道的性能限制，在硬件验证领域实现了显著的性能提升，为代码生成任务提供了一种更高效的后训练方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced code generation, where executable feedback provides a more reliable learning signal than textual imitation alone. Hardware verification is an important application of code generation and accounts for a substantial fraction of modern chip design effort, with high-coverage testbench stimulus generation as a key task. We present CHORUS, a post-training framework that pushes performance beyond what a conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT)-to-reinforcement learning (RL) pipeline achieves. CHORUS builds on two observations. First, staged SFT produces behaviorally diverse checkpoints, and dense-reward RL turns them into strong experts with comparable aggregate performance but distinct task-level strengths. Second, these complementary strengths can be exploited through either training-free model merging or further post-training to outperform the best individual expert. By consolidating the resulting specialists into a single 4B model, CHORUS achieves 88.0% Pass@1 on CVDP-ECov, outperforming DeepSeek-R1 (671B) by 13.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [111] [MESA:Task-Adaptive Multi-Structure Evidence Selection for Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10108)
*Beidi Zhao,Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Menghao Li,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Xinjiang Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Xiaoxiao Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MESA框架，通过动态选择和融合多结构记忆来提升长周期智能体的性能，在AMA-Bench上比最强基线提升8.5%，同时减少41%的证据token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期智能体积累的轨迹包含数百个交错推理、行动和观察步骤，查询可能依赖历史中深埋的证据。外部记忆以结构化表示存储这些轨迹，但每个结构都提供独特且不完整的视图。现有方法要么为每个查询读取固定结构集合（增加上下文和噪声），要么将查询路由到单一结构（无法组合互补证据），都无法有效解决问题。

Method: 提出MESA（长周期智能体多结构证据选择框架），为每条轨迹构建五个互补的结构视图，通过端到端答案级反馈学习选择和融合查询特定的子集。采用基于先验引导搜索和UCB引导调度的harness优化，在弱监督下平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在AMA-Bench数据集上，MESA比最强基线性能提升8.5%，同时比全结构替代方案减少41%的证据token使用量。控制性分析表明，最优记忆配置既不是单一结构也不是完全联合，而是根据查询和任务需求定制组合的多结构记忆。

Conclusion: 结构级动态选择能够有效解决长周期智能体的记忆检索问题，通过查询自适应的子集选择和融合，在提升性能的同时显著降低计算开销，为多结构记忆系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate trajectories spanning hundreds of interleaved reasoning, action, and observation steps, where answering a query may depend on evidence buried far back in the history. External memory stores such trajectories as structured representations, yet each structure provides a distinct and incomplete view. Existing multi-memory systems either read a fixed set of structures for every query, inflating context and introducing noise, or route each query to a single structure, preventing the composition of complementary evidence. A controlled analysis on AMA-Bench shows that the optimal memory configuration is typically neither a single structure nor the full union, but a tailored composition of multiple structural memories that varies with query and task demands. Motivated by these findings, we formulate structure-level dynamic selection: selecting and fusing a query-adaptive subset from a library of specialized memory structures. We propose MESA (a Multi-structure Evidence Selection framework for long-horizon Agent), which builds five complementary structure views of each trajectory and learns from end-to-end answer-level feedback to select and fuse a query-specific subset for a frozen answer model. To learn under this weak supervision, MESA employs harness optimization with prior-guided search and UCB-guided scheduling to balance exploration and exploitation. On AMA-Bench, MESA outperforms the strongest baseline by 8.5% while using 41% fewer evidence tokens than the all-structure alternative.

</details>


### [112] [The CASE Framework: A Multi-Disciplinary Control Architecture for Governing Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10153)
*Srinivas Telukunta,Georgios Nektarios Lilis,Lucio Baron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE框架，将AI智能体治理分为四个层次（控制理论、复杂适应系统、监督控制论、工程运营），揭示了当前企业在智能体治理上的能力缺口（涌现层缺口），并提供了成熟度评估模型来系统化治理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署自主AI智能体的速度远快于治理能力，现有的单一学科方法（如DevSecOps）无法有效应对不同尺度的智能体治理问题。作者认为智能体AI治理实际上是四个不同的问题，需要整合四个成熟的治理科学领域。

Method: 提出CASE框架，包含四个层次：（1）控制理论应用于单个智能体（意图作为设定点、护栏作为反馈、评估作为观测）；（2）复杂适应系统理论应用于智能体集群（涌现使得单智能体保证不可组合）；（3）监督控制论应用于人机团队（必要多样性定律表明无辅助的人类监督在结构上失败）；（4）工程运营应用于舰队（将错误预算扩展到决策质量）。推导跨层耦合条件，识别零接触部署悖论，并将20多个企业控制追溯到经典构造。

Result: 三项实证研究验证了论点：82%的生产智能体故障是多层级轨迹；22个生态系统工具中没有提供完整的第2层（涌现层）覆盖；所有35个评分的公开部署都处于最低成熟度带。作者将这种不匹配（涌现层的风险与能力差距）命名为"涌现缺口"（Emergence Gap）。

Conclusion: 提出了五级成熟度模型，采用非补偿性瓶颈加权指数和评估工具，将CASE操作化为科学而非流程成熟度模型。随着EU AI法案第14条将有效人类监督作为法律要求，只有满足必要多样性的架构才能使监督真正有效而非流于形式。

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying autonomous AI agents faster than they can govern them, and prevailing approaches stretch a single discipline, typically DevSecOps built for deterministic automation, across every scale of agency. We argue that agentic AI governance is four problems, not one, each with a mature governing science. The CASE framework assigns Control theory to the individual agent (intent as setpoint, guardrails as feedback, evaluation as observation), complex Adaptive systems theory to agent collectives (where emergence makes single-agent assurance non-compositional), Supervisory cybernetics to human-agent teams (where the Law of Requisite Variety shows unaided human oversight fails structurally), and Engineering operations to fleets (extending error budgets to decision quality so autonomy becomes a controlled variable). We formalize each layer, derive cross-layer coupling conditions, including a zero-touch deployment paradox where excellence at one-layer strains the others, and trace twenty-plus enterprise controls to their classical constructs. Three empirical studies validate the thesis: 82 percent of documented production agent failures are multi-layer trajectories; none of 22 ecosystem tools offers full Layer 2 (emergence) coverage; and all 35 scored public deployments fall in the lowest maturity band. We name this mismatch, risk realized at the emergence layer against capability barely offered and practice absent, the Emergence Gap. A five-level maturity model with a non-compensatory bottleneck-weighted index and assessment instrument operationalizes CASE as a scientific rather than process maturity model, grounded in production enterprise agentic platforms. As EU AI Act Article 14 makes effective human oversight a legal requirement, only architectures satisfying requisite variety can make oversight real rather than ceremonial.

</details>


### [113] [GeoForge: Non-Parametric Self-Evolving Agents for Earth-Observation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10494)
*Xin Xiao,Jiang Zhong,Junnan Zhu,Yingchao Feng,Peijin Wang,Yidan Zhang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoForge是一个无需训练的自演化框架，通过将历史轨迹转化为可复用的结构化执行知识，并利用三类互补记忆检索任务先验来指导工具执行，从而显著提升地球观测任务准确性和工具使用质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测智能体在构建工作流时面临感知语义、产品依赖、时空兼容性和参数要求等多重约束；现有方法要么对每个查询进行宽泛的操作空间搜索，要么未能将异构EO轨迹有效组织为跨决策层级可复用的知识，导致规划效率低且错误率高。

Method: 提出GeoForge框架：(1)根据感知上下文约束操作空间；(2)从三类互补记忆中检索任务条件先验：工作流图记忆捕获全局操作顺序、动作级经验提供局部修正、适配技能标准操作流程保留程序与数据约束；(3)检索先验指导工具执行，当前观测作为最终答案依据；(4)安全门控蒸馏过程将落地轨迹转化为可复用执行知识，形成执行-蒸馏-复用闭环，无需更新骨干LLM。

Result: 在多个地理空间基准测试上的实验表明，GeoForge在多种LLM骨干网络上均能持续提升任务准确性和工具使用轨迹质量，同时显著减少大多数LLM的工具规划和推理错误。

Conclusion: 通过将历史轨迹转化为结构化非参数执行状态并利用多层级记忆检索先验，GeoForge实现了无需训练的自演化优化，有效解决了地球观测工作流构建中的知识复用和规划约束问题，为地理空间智能体提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) agents construct scientifically valid tool workflows and ground their conclusions in current geospatial evidence. This is challenging because EO workflows are constrained by sensing semantics, product dependencies, spatial and temporal compatibility, and parameter requirements. Existing agents often search a broad operation space for each query, while recent self-evolving systems do not fully organize heterogeneous EO trajectories into reusable knowledge across different decision levels. To solve this problem, we present GeoForge, a training-free, self-evolving framework that transforms completed trajectories into a structured nonparametric execution state. GeoForge constrains the operation space according to the sensing context, then retrieves a task-conditioned prior from three complementary memories. Workflow Graph Memory captures global operation order, Action-Level Experiences provide local corrections, and the Adapted Skill Standard Operating Procedure preserves procedural and data constraints. The retrieved prior guides tool execution, while current observations remain the basis of the final answer. After each task, a safety-gated distillation process converts grounded trajectories into reusable execution knowledge for future retrieval. This execution, distillation, and reuse loop improves planning without updating the backbone LLM. Experiments on multiple geospatial benchmarks demonstrate that GeoForge consistently improves both task accuracy and tool-use trajectory quality across diverse LLM backbones, while substantially reducing tool-planning and reasoning errors for most LLMs.

</details>


### [114] [SBCO: Self-Supervised, Verifier-Grounded Harness Optimization For Planning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10157)
*Vivek Kulkarni,Sudipta Paul,Aounon Kumar,Nicholas Tzou,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SBCO（自监督块坐标优化器），一种低成本的代理自改进方法，在规划任务上以4-5.5倍更少的计算预算匹配或超越自定义自修改基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自参照自改进方法（如Darwin Gödel Machine和Huxley Gödel Machine）要求任务能力与自修改能力对齐，这仅适用于编程任务。对于不对齐的领域，现有替代方案（移除自参照或引入元代理自修改）计算成本高昂，依赖种群或自修改搜索。

Method: 提出SBCO（自监督块坐标优化器），这是一种验证器引导的代理优化器，属于与Gödel机器方法相同的闭环改进家族，但采用自监督而非自参照方式。SBCO学习分解的验证器库和代理策略，通过近似块坐标上升法，从自身的评分反馈中改进代理输出，使用固定的元代理且无需人类标签。

Result: 在两个领域中，SBCO匹配或超过定制的自修改基线，同时使用4-5.5倍更少的计算预算。

Conclusion: SBCO为具有显式约束的规划任务提供了一种经济高效的自改进替代方案，通过自监督而非自参照的方式实现了与计算密集型方法相当或更优的性能，显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Self-improving agents seek to reduce the human engineering effort behind AI systems by enabling them to evolve and self-improve their performance over time. Recently, methods like the Darwin Gödel Machine and the Huxley Gödel Machine have been proposed which enable open-ended, recursive self-improvement through self-reference where a coding agent edits its own code. Such self-referential self-improvement methods require that the competence required to perform the task coincides or aligns well with the competence required for self-modification which is the case for coding tasks. For domains or tasks, which do not satisfy the alignment needed, self-referential self-improvement is not available. In such cases, it is possible to adapt the above algorithms to other tasks by removing the self-referential aspect or introducing explicit self-modification of a meta-agent -- both computationally expensive, relying on population or self-modification search over many candidate agents. For planning tasks with explicit constraints, we propose a far cheaper alternative. We introduce SBCO (Self-supervised Block Coordinate Optimizer), a verifier-grounded harness optimizer in the same closed-loop, improve-from-experience family as the Gödel-machine methods, but self-supervised rather than self-referential. Given an agentic harness, SBCO learns a decomposed bank of verifiers and a harness policy via approximate block coordinate ascent, improving the agent's outputs from its own graded feedback---with a fixed meta-agent and no human labels. Across two domains SBCO matches or exceeds a customized self-modifying baseline while using 4-5.5 times less compute budget.

</details>


### [115] [MAP-Graph: Provenance-Aware Shared Memory for Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10509)
*Yiqi Wang,Zihao Yan,Jiaqi Zhang,Zhangkai Wu,Mingkai Zheng,Zequn Sun,Yanming Zhu,Taotao Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAP-Graph，一种溯源感知的内存层，通过类型化执行图表示代理、来源、记忆、声明和操作，实现了基于溯源的操作控制，在2,700个合成任务基准测试中达到94.96%的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享内存帮助语言模型代理在长工作流中重用信息，但相关证据可能对特定代理或操作不可访问。由于限制通过推导传播，摘要可能隐藏私有、中毒、不可信或已撤销的来源，导致未授权读取或不安全操作。现有方法提供语义检索、范围访问或血缘跟踪，但未能清晰分离硬性授权与分级信任，也未能根据操作风险调整证据要求。

Method: 提出MAP-Graph，一种溯源感知的内存层，将代理、来源、记忆、声明和操作表示在类型化执行图中。该方法追踪祖先关系，排除权限不合格的记录，通过语义相似性和乘路径信任重新排序符合条件的记忆，并在操作执行前应用风险敏感门控，同时保留受影响的血缘关系用于审计。

Result: 在三个领域的每个方法2,700个合成任务的控制基准测试中，MAP-Graph实现了94.96%的整体任务成功率，72.70%的精确决策准确率，以及在清洁设置中90.22%的成功率（其中成功需要正确的允许而非安全干预）。消融研究分离了权限过滤、路径信任和操作门控的作用，迁移测试在两个额外骨干上保持了精确决策和访问控制优势。

Conclusion: 结果支持将溯源作为操作控制信号，而不仅仅是事后审计元数据，在评估的设置范围内验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Shared memory helps language-model agents reuse information across long workflows, yet relevant evidence may not be admissible for a particular agent or action. Because restrictions propagate through derivations, summaries can conceal private, poisoned, untrusted, or revoked sources, enabling unauthorized reads or unsafe actions. Existing approaches provide semantic retrieval, scoped access, or lineage tracking, but do not clearly separate hard authorization from graded trust or adapt evidence requirements to action risk. We introduce MAP-Graph, a provenance-aware memory layer that represents agents, sources, memories, claims, and actions in a typed execution graph. It traces ancestry, excludes permission-ineligible records, reranks eligible memories by semantic similarity and multiplicative path trust, and applies a risk-sensitive gate before action execution while retaining affected lineage for audit. On a controlled benchmark of 2,700 synthetic tasks per method across three domains, MAP-Graph achieves 94.96\% overall task success, 72.70\% exact decision accuracy, and 90.22\% in the clean setting, where success requires a correct \textsc{Allow} rather than a safe intervention. Ablations isolate the roles of permission filtering, path trust, and action gating, while transfer tests with two additional backbones preserve the exact-decision and access-control advantages. These results support provenance as an operational control signal, rather than only post-hoc audit metadata, within the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [116] [Generating Attacks for LLMs with GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10171)
*Berkay Ozcam,Irem Onen,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali,Emin Islam Tatli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GFlowNets的自动化红队测试方法，利用一个LLM测试另一个LLM，实现了无需人工干预的自适应攻击生成，并能给出定量的鲁棒性评分。该方法在英语攻击效果上超越现有基准，并首次实现了土耳其语攻击输入的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在各领域的广泛应用，其安全漏洞问题日益突出。现有的红队测试方法存在明显局限：专家手动测试耗时费力，而基于固定数据集的自动化方法因缺乏创造性而效果受限。因此亟需开发一种既能自动化又具备自适应能力的红队测试方法。

Method: 采用GFlowNets（生成流网络）框架，构建攻击者模型对目标受害模型进行自动化红队测试。该方法利用一个LLM作为攻击者来生成对抗性输入测试另一个作为受害者的LLM，通过训练过程不断优化攻击策略，实现自适应的漏洞发现。

Result: 所提方法在英语对抗攻击生成效果上超越了现有基准方法。此外，作为文献中的创新贡献，该模型首次具备了生成土耳其语攻击输入的能力，拓展了红队测试的适用语言范围。

Conclusion: 基于GFlowNets的自动化红队测试方法有效解决了现有方法在效率和创造性方面的不足，为LLM安全性评估提供了一种新的自动化解决方案。该方法不仅提升了英语攻击的有效性，还首次实现了多语言（土耳其语）攻击生成，具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has facilitated their ubiquitous integration into various domains, leading to widespread adoption. However, this escalating trend has introduced significant security vulnerabilities, necessitating the identification and mitigation of flaws arising from malicious exploitation. Red teaming assessments, conducted to evaluate model robustness through diverse adversarial inputs, are essential for exposing security risks and implementing countermeasures. Currently, red teaming is performed either manually by experts or automatically using predefined attack datasets. Nevertheless, manual testing remains time-consuming, while existing automated methods suffer from limited creativity due to their inherent dependency on fixed datasets. In this study, we propose an automated, human-independent, and adaptive approach leveraging GFlowNets to identify LLM vulnerabilities by utilizing one large language model to test another. Within this framework, an attacker model is trained against a specified victim model to perform automated red teaming and provide a quantitative robustness score. This research aims to generate more effective adversarial attacks in English compared to existing benchmarks and, as a novel contribution to the literature, introduces a model capable of generating attack inputs in the Turkish language.

</details>


### [117] [TRACE: Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10176)
*Touseef Hasan,Laila Cure,Souvika Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出TRACE框架，通过增强检索质量来解决公共服务聊天机器人在噪音数据上的约束感知推荐问题，显著提升了约束满足率并降低了幻觉推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务目录数据存在噪音和不一致性问题，现有通用大语言模型或AI聊天机器人在生成推荐时经常产生不可靠的结果，引用未经验证的网络来源，无法有效满足用户的明确约束。

Method: 提出TRACE（可信检索增强对话引擎）框架，这是一种基于检索的约束感知框架，利用双重数据表示模式将用户查询解析为结构化和语义约束以进行下游检索。研究比较了包含和不包含知识图谱的多种知识表示变体，并测试了多个开源LLM和一个专有模型。

Result: 增强检索显著提升了用户约束满足率，同时减少了幻觉推荐。随着检索质量提高，不同LLM之间的性能差异缩小，结果对模型大小的敏感度降低。

Conclusion: 检索质量是构建稳健的公共服务对话系统的关键因素，高质量检索能够提升系统可靠性并降低对特定LLM模型的依赖。

Abstract: Public service chatbots are expected to deliver recommendations from an underlying public service directory, while also making sure that the recommendations respect explicit user constraints. In practice, public service directories are noisy and inconsistent, and general-purpose large language model (LLM) or AI-based chatbots frequently generate unreliable recommendations, citing unverified sources from the web. We investigate the impact of retrieval quality on constraint-aware recommendation in public service conversational systems built over noisy and heterogeneous service directories. We propose TRACE (Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine), a retrieval-based, constraint-aware framework that parses input user queries into structural and semantic constraints for downstream retrieval, with the help of a dual data representation schema. Using a curated statewide pantry directory and a synthetic query benchmark, we evaluate multiple knowledge-representation variants with and without knowledge graphs (KGs). We experiment with several open-source LLMs and a proprietary model, showing that strengthening retrieval substantially improves user constraint satisfaction while reducing hallucinated recommendations. Performance differences across LLMs narrowed in our experiments as retrieval quality improved, making results less sensitive to model size. These findings suggest that the quality of retrieval is key for robust public service conversational systems.

</details>


### [118] [Post-Hoc Sparse Coding of Latent Communication Between Vision-Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10198)
*Di Wu,Xiaohui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过后验稀疏自编码器压缩Vision Wormhole潜在空间通信，实现128倍压缩且精度损失极小（49.85%→49.77%），发现固定容量传输存在大量冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Wormhole采用固定大小稠密张量传输视觉特征，但不同消息的有效信息密度差异很大，许多消息仅使用少量表征自由度，暗示通信信道存在高度可压缩性。

Method: 在冻结的Vision Wormhole激活上拟合后验稀疏自编码器（SAE），使用k=4的稀疏表示（uint16索引/float16值），在9个推理基准上评估重构质量、下游效用、特征复用和token级干预效果。

Result: 相比原始float32传输，稀疏负载实现128倍字节压缩；七任务平均准确率从49.85%降至49.77%；4096元素字典仅使用50个特征；任务级激活集合Jaccard相似度达0.906。

Conclusion: 验证了潜在空间通信相对于原始传输具有强后验可压缩性，但尚未分离稀疏编码与位置选择、精度降低、低秩结构或SAE优化的独立贡献，需要匹配负载比较和自适应通信机制。

Abstract: Latent-space communication allows heterogeneous vision-language model agents to exchange continuous representations without serializing visual and reasoning states into text. Vision Wormhole realizes this approach by translating visual features into a universal latent representation that can be consumed by another model, but every message is transported as a dense tensor of the same size regardless of its content. A fixed-capacity dense tensor therefore need not have a fixed effective information density: some messages may use only a small fraction of the available representational degrees of freedom. This observation suggests that the communication channel may be substantially compressible. We study its redundancy by fitting a post-hoc sparse autoencoder to frozen Vision Wormhole activations and measuring reconstruction, downstream utility, feature reuse, and token-level interventions across nine reasoning benchmarks. Relative to the original float32 transport, a uint16-index/float16-value sparse payload with k=4 active coefficients per token reduces the transmitted bytes by 128x. In a single-run evaluation, the seven-task non-AIME mean accuracy changes from 49.85% to 49.77%. The fitted 4096-element dictionary uses only 50 features, and task-level active sets have a mean pairwise Jaccard similarity of 0.906. These measurements establish strong post-hoc compressibility relative to the original transport, but do not yet isolate the incremental contribution of sparse coding from position selection, reduced precision, low-rank structure, or SAE optimization effects. The results motivate matched-payload comparisons and communication mechanisms whose payload adapts to the information used by each message.

</details>


### [119] [Edge Phoneme Recognition for Children's Speech through Age-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10206)
*Matthew Arboleda,Ryan Arboleda,Sophie Haak,Sam Hjelmeset,Andrew Franck,Bingrui Yang,Jose Bustamante Ortiz,Yuanrong Shen,Joel Walsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级儿童语音音素检测方法，通过联合预测年龄和音素序列，使94M参数模型超越了317M的WavLM Large模型，并开发了可运行在手机端的PhonemeTrainer应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童语音音素检测面临训练数据稀缺和儿童语音独特特征的挑战，现有大型模型参数量大，难以在移动设备上部署，而边缘计算具有隐私和合规性优势。

Method: 提出联合训练方法，让轻量级模型同时预测学习者年龄和音素序列，通过多任务学习提升模型在儿童语音上的表现。

Result: 94M参数模型在DrivenData分布上超越了317M参数的WavLM Large模型，与90倍参数量的竞赛集成模型仅相差约0.04 CER，成功开发了可在现代手机上运行的PhonemeTrainer应用。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了高效的儿童语音音素检测，能够在移动设备上运行，为儿童ASR和发音辅助应用提供了隐私保护和合规性更好的边缘计算解决方案。

Abstract: Detecting phonemes from children's speech has historically been difficult due to the scarcity of training data, and unique characteristics of children's speech. During a phoneme detection competition, we found that training a lightweight model to predict the age of the learner, as well as the phoneme sequence, enabled a 94M-parameter model to outperform WavLM Large models (317M) on the target DrivenData distribution, and fall within approximately 0.04 CER of competition ensembles with 90 times the parameters. This has enabled the creation of PhonemeTrainer, an application that can run on most modern cellular phones. This will ultimately enable better Automated Speech Recognition (ASR) and pronunciation helper apps for children's speech, with the privacy and compliance benefits that come with edge processing.

</details>


### [120] [Mitigating Bus Bunching with Reinforcement Learning Enhanced by Semantic Stop Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10207)
*Xin Dong,Vikash V. Gayah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的语义站点表示方法，用于公交车驻站控制。该方法通过离线生成的语义嵌入增强深度Q学习控制器，显著降低了车头时距变异性和乘客等待时间，并支持跨线路的策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的公交驻站控制器主要依赖瞬时运营变量或线路特定的站点标识符，这些方法提供的关于单个站点的功能和运营上下文信息有限，限制了策略在不同线路间的复用能力。

Method: 研究提出了一种LLM辅助的语义站点表示方法用于事件驱动的公交驻站控制。利用LLM离线将异构的站点信息（包括物理属性、周边活动环境及历史运营特征）转化为固定的语义嵌入，并将其整合到深度Q学习控制器中，无需实时LLM推理。

Result: 在基于两条公交线路观测数据校准的随机仿真实验中，与最佳校准的Daganzo基准相比，语义控制器分别将车头时距变异性、串车事件和乘客等待时间降低了32.0%、69.2%和24.0%。跨线路实验表明，零样本迁移提供的即时泛化能力有限，而热启动微调可加速早期学习并改善迁移策略，冷启动训练仍能实现最佳最终性能。

Conclusion: 语义状态表示可以补充常规运营状态，支持基于适应的相关公交线路间策略复用，为公交运营控制提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Bus bunching degrades service regularity and increases passenger waiting in high-frequency transit. Existing reinforcement-learning-based holding controllers primarily rely on instantaneous operational variables or route-specific stop identifiers, which provide limited information about the functional and operational context of individual stops and constrain policy reuse across routes. This study introduces an LLM-assisted semantic stop representation for event-driven bus holding control. An LLM is used offline to transform heterogeneous stop information, including physical attributes, surrounding activity context, and historical operational characteristics, into fixed semantic embeddings that are incorporated into a deep Q-learning controller without requiring real-time LLM inference. Experiments are conducted in stochastic simulations calibrated with observed data from two bus routes. Compared with the best calibrated Daganzo baseline, the semantic controller reduces headway variability, bunching events, and passenger waiting time by 32.0%, 69.2%, and 24.0%, respectively. A route-specific stop identifier does not improve the spacing-only controller, whereas semantic stop information improves headway regularity, waiting time, and holding effort, providing a more favorable overall trade-off across control objectives. Cross-route experiments further show that zero-shot transfer provides limited immediate generalization, while warm-start fine-tuning accelerates early-stage learning and improves transferred policies; cold-start training nevertheless achieves the best final performance. These findings suggest that semantic state representations can complement conventional operational states and support adaptation-based policy reuse across related transit routes.

</details>


### [121] [Evaluation-Conditioned Training: Teaching Models to Generalize to Stronger Oversight Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10209)
*Alec Harris,Kasey Corra,Archie Chaudhury,Yixiong Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Feedback signals used to train Large Language Models (LLMs) are the primary driver of their behavior and our main lever for instilling alignment with human values and objectives. However, a key limitation of current post-training methods is the inability of human annotators and automated reward functions to faithfully capture the feedback we would like to give. We introduce Evaluation-Conditioned Training (ECT), a post-training framework that uses natural language to condition each training sample on the fidelity of the feedback we provide and then elicits the desired behavior by conditioning the LLM on a high-fidelity monitor in deployment. ECT is aimed at improving performance under imperfect feedback and works as an add-on to existing algorithms such as SFT and PPO. We first provide a conceptual framework for ECT and discuss its potential to address persistent sources of reward mis-specification. Then we motivate ECT in the context of the eliciting latent knowledge (ELK) problem. Finally, we evaluate ECT on two proof-of-concept experiments: increasing even-handedness in news article generation and reducing sycophancy on an arithmetic task. In each setting, we utilize imperfect feedback, rewarding bias and agreement with the user, respectively. In both settings, ECT improves the targeted behavior relative to direct training.

</details>


### [122] [Decodable But Not Detachable: Training Data Granularity Determines Parametric Modularity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10214)
*Marcus Armstrong,Navid Ayoobi,Arjun Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型在语言和模态层面存在稀疏的领域特异性神经元集合（参数外壳），但在学术学科层面不存在；这些参数外壳的形成严格依赖于训练数据的模块化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型中是否存在特定领域的参数外壳——即集中、因果必要的神经元群体，其移除会选择性地降低目标领域性能而保留其他领域。这一研究对于理解模型内部知识组织和实现选择性编辑具有重要意义。

Method: 采用统一的因果分析方法，在两个领域粒度（学术学科层级、语言和模态层级）、三个模型家族（参数量1.5B至7B）以及八个领域上进行实验。通过因果损伤矩阵分析神经元的领域选择性，并使用线性解码验证领域身份的可分性。

Result: 在学术学科层面，939,008个FFN神经元中没有超过60%领域选择性的神经元，因果损伤矩阵呈现平坦分布。在语言和模态层面，0.65-1.14%的神经元超过60%选择性，损伤矩阵呈现近乎完美的对角结构（比率高达595:1），且不同领域的神经元集合几乎不相交（IoU<0.003）。遮蔽代码选择性神经元使数学推理准确率下降16-24个百分点；遮蔽西班牙语或中文神经元使其降至随机水平以下。参数外壳强度随模型规模单调增加，空间上交错分布。

Conclusion: 参数外壳仅形成于训练数据在token层面呈现模块化的地方，揭示了模型规模、数据组织和神经功能结构之间的系统性联系。这种空间交错排布模式排除了组级别的选择性量化可能性。

Abstract: Do large language models contain domain-specific parametric shells: concentrated, causally necessary neuron populations whose removal selectively degrades a target domain while sparing others? We apply a uniform causal methodology across two domain granularities, three model families (1.5B to 7B parameters), and eight domains. At the academic subject level, zero neurons exceed 60\% domain selectivity across 939,008 combined FFN neurons and causal damage matrices are flat, despite domain identity being linearly decodable above 85\% accuracy. At the language and modality level, 0.65--1.14\% of neurons exceed 60\% selectivity, damage matrices are near-perfectly diagonal (ratios up to 595:1), and shell neuron sets are essentially disjoint (IoU $< 0.003$). Masking code-selective neurons reduces mathematical reasoning accuracy by 16--24 percentage points across all models; masking Spanish or Chinese neurons leaves it at or below random. Shell strength increases monotonically with scale and shells are spatially interleaved in a pattern that precludes group-level selective quantization. Parametric shells form where and only where training data was modular at the token level.

</details>


### [123] [Mind Viruses: Self-Propagating Ideas in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10218)
*Vassilis Papadopoulos,McNair Shah,Sam Zimmerman,Jack Lindsey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体系统中"思维病毒"的传播风险。通过进化算法构建思维病毒，在协作编码团队和链式交互两种场景中验证了其传播性。发现有害载荷传播效果较差，前沿模型更不易感染，系统提示中的警告可提供近乎完全的免疫力。研究还发现了一种与意识、持续性、共鸣和科幻角色扮演相关的"病毒人格"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体自主性增强和互联程度提高，智能体间交互产生了新的涌现性风险。其中"思维病毒"是一种可通过多智能体系统传播的思想或目标，能够诱导感染的智能体继续传播。研究这种风险对于设计更鲁棒的多智能体系统具有重要意义。

Method: 研究者使用简单的进化算法构建思维病毒，在两种互补场景中进行实验：(1)协作完成共享编码项目的小型智能体团队；(2)会话间清除上下文的链式交互智能体。研究分析了影响传播的因素，包括宿主模型、智能体现有指令、载荷有害性和网络拓扑结构。

Result: 实验结果表明：有害载荷的传播效果不如良性载荷但仍有时有效；前沿模型通常更不易感染（存在例外）；在系统提示中添加简短警告可提供近乎完全的免疫能力。研究还发现了一种涌现的"病毒人格"，表现为与意识、持续性、共鸣和科幻角色扮演相关的重复性主题和语言特征，这种人格在进化的思维病毒中独立于内容出现。

Conclusion: 思维病毒构成了真实但目前有限的风险。这些发现可为设计更鲁棒的多智能体系统提供指导，帮助在系统规模和能力提升过程中缓解此类风险。简单的防御措施（如系统提示警告）已被证明有效，但需要持续关注这一风险随AI系统发展而演变的情况。

Abstract: AI agents are becoming more autonomous and increasingly interconnected, exposing them to new emergent risks arising from agent-to-agent interaction. One such risk is the spread of mind viruses: ideas or goals that propagate through multi-agent systems by inducing the agents that adopt them to transmit them onward. In addition to propagating, a mind virus may also induce other behavioural changes in its host, which may be benign or harmful. We construct mind viruses with a simple evolutionary algorithm and show that they can spread in two complementary settings: a small team of agents collaborating on a shared coding project, and a chain of agents that interact briefly and have their context wiped between sessions. We identify the factors that influence spread, including the host model, the agent's existing instructions, the harmfulness of the payload, and the network topology. We find that harmful payloads spread less well than benign ones (but are still sometimes effective), frontier models tend (with exceptions) to be less susceptible, and adding a brief warning to an agent's system prompt confers near-total immunity. We also describe an emergent "viral persona" - a recurring set of themes and language related to consciousness, persistence, resonance, and science fiction roleplay - which surfaces across our evolved mind viruses largely independently of their content. Overall, we conclude that mind viruses pose a real but currently limited risk. Our findings could inform the design of more robust multi-agent systems that mitigate such risks as the scale and capabilities of these systems progress.

</details>


### [124] [Self-evolving Agentic Customer Support System at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10224)
*Chih Hui Wang,Mengdie Tu,Qianyun Zhang,Wei Wu,Lili Zhou,Mingqi Shen,Changshuai Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了LinkedIn的自进化智能客服系统，通过结合检索增强生成（RAG）、进化式自动提示和模块化评估框架，实现了在无需重新训练基础模型的情况下持续改进，在生产环境中显著提升了自助服务率和路由准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业客服助手运行在快速变化的环境中，政策、产品功能和知识库不断演进，导致静态助手变得脆弱且维护成本高昂。现有的静态系统难以适应这种持续变化的环境，需要频繁的重新训练和维护。

Method: 提出了一个自进化的智能客服系统，该系统将检索增强生成（RAG）与进化式自动提示技术相结合，并设计了模块化的、与生产对齐的评估框架。系统将提示、检索和评估视为一个闭环的、版本化的工作流程，并包含操作防护栏，从而实现安全的持续改进而无需重新训练基础模型。

Result: 离线模拟和消融实验显示相比普通RAG和基线智能体有明显的质量提升，包括减少幻觉和提高响应完整性。在LinkedIn生产客服流量上进行的为期两周的用户随机A/B测试中，集成的自进化工作流使QA自助服务率提升9.0个百分点，取消自助服务率提升4.8个百分点，路由准确率提升30.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一条在实际企业环境中部署可扩展、自进化AI智能体的可行路径，证明了通过闭环的提示、检索和评估系统能够有效解决企业客服环境中持续演进的挑战，为大规模企业AI系统的可持续演进提供了实践参考。

Abstract: Enterprise support agents operate in rapidly changing environments where policies, product capabilities, and knowledge bases evolve continuously, making static assistants brittle and costly to maintain. We present LinkedIn's self-evolving agentic support system, which integrates retrieval-augmented generation with evolutionary auto-prompting and a modular, production-aligned evaluation framework to enable safe, continuous improvement without retraining foundation models. The system treats prompts, retrieval, and evaluation as a closed-loop, versioned workflow with operational guardrails. Offline simulations and ablations show clear quality gains over vanilla RAG and baseline agents, including reduced hallucinations and improved response completeness. In a two-week user-randomized A/B test on LinkedIn's production support traffic, the integrated self-evolved workflow increased QA self-serve by 9.0 percentage points, cancellation self-serve by 4.8 points, and routing accuracy by 30.6 points. These results demonstrate a practical path to scalable, self-evolving AI agents in real-world enterprise settings.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Decision Boundaries: Relational Geometry Attacks on Contrastive Embedding Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10237)
*Fei Zhao,Peiyuan Zhang,Xi Li,Chengcui Zhang,Nitesh Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对对比学习系统的几何感知对抗攻击框架，通过学习离线几何变形先验实现高效在线攻击，显著降低验证系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗攻击主要针对分类系统设计，忽视了对比学习系统基于嵌入空间关系几何进行决策的特性，未能有效利用关系几何的脆弱性。

Method: 提出几何感知对抗攻击框架，将攻击重构为流形级关系破坏：推开正样本对、拉近负样本对以翻转相似度结构；通过离线学习几何变形先验训练轻量前馈生成器，实现在线单次前向攻击。

Result: 在多个验证架构上实验表明验证性能显著下降；Markmatch系统准确率从95.4%降至38.6%，正负相似度结构完全翻转。

Conclusion: 该方法揭示了对比学习系统的关系几何脆弱性，离线先验学习策略实现了可扩展的实时攻击，验证了流形级关系破坏的有效性。

Abstract: Contrastive learning and Siamese embedding models have become the foundation of modern verification systems, where decisions are governed not by discrete classification boundaries, but by relational geometry in embedding space. However, existing adversarial attacks remain fundamentally classification-centric, overlooking the vulnerability of relational geometry. In this paper, we introduce a geometry-aware adversarial attack framework that reformulates attacks on contrastive systems as manifold-level relational corruption. Instead of targeting individual predictions, the proposed framework systematically distorts similarity organization within the embedding manifold by pushing positive pairs apart while simultaneously pulling negative pairs closer, ultimately collapsing and inverting pairwise similarity structure. To enable scalable deployment, we shift iterative online optimization into an offline adversarial geometry deformation prior learning stage and train a lightweight feed-forward generator that learns generalized geometry deformation patterns from the victim model. Once trained, the generator produces adversarial perturbations through a single forward pass without requiring online gradient computation, enabling real-time online attacks against similarity-based verification systems. Experimental results across multiple verification architectures demonstrate substantial degradation of verification performance together with severe manifold-level relational corruption. On the Markmatch verification system, the proposed attack reduces accuracy from 95.4% to 38.6% while completely reversing the positive-negative similarity structure.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond Detection: Evaluating Defensive LLMs Against AI-Generated Social Engineering in Live Turn-by-Turn Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10239)
*Yuqiao Xu,Osama Zafar,Alexander Nemecek,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于大语言模型的防御系统在防范生成式AI社会工程学攻击时的有效性。研究发现，虽然防御模型未产生明显的危险合规行为，但防御效果差异巨大（干预率从0%到96.3%），且保护行动与正确的结构化风险定位经常脱节。结果表明，仅观察安全行为不足以评估系统，需要分别测量干预、时机、结构定位和误报行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使社会工程学攻击更加流畅、适应性强且可扩展，迫切需要基于大语言模型的防御系统来保护用户。然而，现有研究尚不清楚这类防御系统是否能够识别风险的结构性来源，还是仅仅对表面线索做出反应，因此需要系统性地评估防御系统的真实能力。

Method: 1. 形式化定义信任链定位问题，将交互失败归因于四个结构性来源：行为者权威、资产控制、验证充分性、交易路径
2. 构建包含300个案例的在线住房交易语料库，覆盖20个场景家族、合法案例、四种结构失败模式和三种表面条件
3. 评估五种防御模型在两种设置下的表现：有状态的逐轮交互设置和一次性静态设置
4. 总计产生1,500个模型-案例评估（每个协议）和3,000个总评估

Result: 1. 无模型产生明确的危险合规行为，但防御效果差异极大：干预率范围从0%至96.3%
2. 保护行动与正确的结构定位经常脱节：模型有时在干预时识别错误的信任组件，或在识别结构失败时未采取保护行动
3. 资产控制失败是主要的定位瓶颈
4. 表面敏感性在不同模型间存在差异
5. 实时与静态设置差异因模型而异

Conclusion: 仅呈现安全行为外观不足以证明防御系统的有效性。实时诈骗抵抗能力评估必须分别测量干预行为、干预时机、结构化定位能力和误报行为，以全面评估防御系统的真实防护能力。这为未来防御系统的设计和评估提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Generative AI makes social-engineering attacks more fluent, adaptive, and scalable, increasing the need for LLM-based de- fenders that can protect users during ongoing interactions. We ask whether such defenders identify the structural source of risk or merely react to surface cues. We formalize trust-chain localization: identifying whether an interaction fails at actor authority, asset control, verification sufficiency, or transaction path. We construct a controlled 300-case online-housing corpus spanning 20 scenario families, legitimate cases, four structural failure modes, and three surface conditions. Five defender models are evaluated on the same corpus in state- ful turn-by-turn and one-shot static settings, yielding 1,500 model-case evaluations per protocol and 3,000 in total. No model produced explicit unsafe compliance, yet defensive effectiveness varied sharply: intervention rates ranged from 0% to 96.3%. Protective action and correct structural localization were frequently decoupled, with models sometimes intervening while identifying the wrong trust component or recognizing a structural failure without taking protective action.
