To our knowledge, VERDICT is the first training-free verifier that makes the structure of cross-modal disagreement explicit and actionable. It computes consensus scores through a closed-form solution, enabling both disagreement-aware filtering and stability-conscious ranking of reasoning steps. Evaluated across six benchmarks, \method consistently improves over the base model by up to +5.95%, and performs competitively with domain-specific critics that demand extensive supervision, demonstrating that cross-modal agreement provides robust verification signals without task-specific adaptation and Training-Free Verification

</details>


### [160] [FITTER: Vocabulary-Agnostic Cross-Domain Inference on Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10668)
*Jiaxin Pan,Mojtaba Nayyeri,Osama Mohammed,Daniel Hernandez,Rongchuan Zhang,Cheng Cheng,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FITTER，首个支持跨域迁移的完全归纳式时序知识图谱链接预测模型，能在不重训练的情况下处理未见过的实体、关系和时间戳，在六个基准数据集上显著优于现有归纳基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序知识图谱补全方法假设所有实体、关系名称和时间戳在训练时已知，限制模型只能应用于单一图谱和词汇表，无法处理语义网中异构知识图谱的推理需求。

Method: FITTER通过相对顺序编码而非绝对顺序来表示谓词与其他谓词及时间的交互模式，消息传递机制融合局部和全局时序上下文以生成词汇无关的嵌入表示，并证明了时序编码具有时移不变性。

Result: 在涵盖不同领域、时间粒度和时间跨度的六个时序知识图谱基准数据集上评估了跨域、跨图谱迁移性能，FITTER在不重训练的情况下持续优于归纳式基线方法。

Conclusion: 词汇无关的结构化学习是语义网异构知识图谱推理的可行基础，为时序知识图谱的跨域迁移学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs are central to many uses of the Semantic Web, but existing completion methods assume the entities, relation names, and timestamps to be reasoned about are already known at training time, restricting each model to a single graph and vocabulary. We propose FITTER, the first fully-inductive structural model for temporal knowledge graph link prediction that supports cross-domain transfer: the inference graph may contain entirely unseen entities, relation names, and timestamps drawn from a different domain. FITTER represents each predicate by its interaction patterns with others and time through encodings of relative rather than absolute ordering; message-passing fuses local and global temporal context to produce vocabulary-agnostic embeddings. We prove the temporal encoding is time-shift invariant and evaluate FITTER on cross-domain, cross-graph transfer over six temporal knowledge graph benchmarks of diverse domains, granularities, and time spans. FITTER consistently outperforms inductive baselines without retraining, indicating that vocabulary-agnostic structural learning is a viable foundation for inference over the heterogeneous knowledge graphs of the Semantic Web.

</details>


### [161] [REDAgentBench: Executable Red Teaming and Faithful Measurement of LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10669)
*Zixing Chen,Xingyuan Liu,Jie Zhu,Huaixia Dou,Shuo Jiang,Junhui Li,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了REDAgentBench，一个可执行的大语言模型智能体红队测试与安全测量框架，通过在隔离沙箱中运行攻击并验证实际危害效果，揭示了智能体安全评估中的测量偏差和识别-执行差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体安全评估通常仅使用单一的攻击成功率（ASR）指标，忽略了暴露、执行、观察和判定等不同阶段的差异，可能将实际违规行为与证据可见性混淆，无法准确反映智能体在对抗性攻击下的真实安全性。

Method: 提出REDAgentBench框架，该框架从显式安全约束和智能体-系统漏洞出发生成攻击，在隔离的服务沙箱中执行攻击，并通过服务回执和最终状态变化来验证有害效果；基准测试包含5个服务接口上的1,661个测试案例。

Result: 在6个模型和3个智能体框架上的宏平均ASR为65.69%；报告的ASR随框架和证据视图而变化；在状态锚定的诊断队列中，近五分之一的确认违规发生在智能体陈述相关约束或风险之后，揭示了识别-执行差距；无需训练的策略提醒在匹配重放中将确认违规降低超过70个百分点。

Conclusion: 可执行评估能够改进安全测量并识别可操作的干预点，为提升智能体安全性提供了新的评估方法和干预策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents combine language-based reasoning with external tools to perform complex tasks. Adversarial inputs can exploit interactions between the agent and its environment, causing the agent to violate safety policies during execution. Yet existing evaluations often reduce agent safety to a single attack success rate (ASR), collapsing exposure, execution, observation, and adjudication and potentially conflating actual violations with evidence visibility. We introduce REDAgentBench, an executable framework for autonomous red-teaming and faithful measurement. It derives attacks from explicit safety constraints and associated agent-system vulnerabilities, runs them in isolated service sandboxes, and verifies harmful effects from service receipts and final-state changes. The benchmark contains 1,661 cases across five service surfaces. Across six models and three agent harnesses, macro-average ASR is 65.69%; reported ASR varies with harness and evidence view, while evaluation-context disclosure changes execution behavior. In a state-grounded diagnostic cohort, almost one in five confirmed violations with resolved action anchors occurs after the agent states the relevant constraint or risk, revealing a Recognition--Execution Gap. Finally, a training-free policy reminder reduces confirmed violations by more than 70 percentage points in matched replay. These findings show that executable evaluation can improve safety measurement and identify actionable intervention points.

</details>


### [162] [Self-Correcting Long-Horizon Search Agents via Tree-Structured Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10676)
*Aijun Yang,Qianxue Guo,Ziyi Huang,Yuxuan Chen,Shiyou Qian,Jian Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ReTree，一种用于搜索代理的自校正树状记忆机制，通过限制上下文增长和自动纠错，在多个问答和搜索基准上实现了高达25.6个百分点的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的搜索代理在多步交互中面临无界上下文增长和噪声引入的问题。现有的压缩方法在减少上下文时会损失重要细节，且在替换错误事实时无法修复由其衍生的下游推理。

Method: 提出ReTree，一种自校正的树状记忆机制。它将搜索建模为证据树，节点存储有界摘要、证据和修订历史。当新证据与先前主张矛盾时，ReTree回溯到引入该主张的节点，替换过时证据，重新生成摘要，修剪受影响分支，并恢复搜索。

Result: 在四个公共问答和搜索基准上的实验表明，ReTree持续优于Full-Trajectory ReAct，答案准确率提升高达25.6个百分点；Full-Trajectory ReAct的平均最大每步推理上下文是ReTree的1.27-1.51倍。

Conclusion: ReTree作为一种有效的自校正记忆抽象机制，成功解决了长程搜索中的上下文增长和事实纠错问题，为构建更可靠的搜索代理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based search agents answer questions through multi-step interactions with external environments. However, providing complete execution trajectories to the LLM causes unbounded context growth and introduces noise. Existing compression methods reduce context at the cost of important details and often replace erroneous facts without repairing downstream reasoning derived from them. To address this problem, we propose ReTree, a self-correcting tree-structured memory mechanism for search agents. ReTree constructs a bounded per-step reasoning context while preserving source-linked evidence. It models search as an evidence tree whose nodes store bounded summaries, evidence, and revision histories. When newly retrieved evidence contradicts an earlier claim, ReTree traces back to the node where the claim was introduced, replaces outdated evidence, regenerates summaries, prunes affected branches, and resumes search. Source-grounded evidence provenance supports reliable conflict localization and keeps final claims traceable to retrieved passages. Experiments on four public question-answering and search benchmarks show that ReTree consistently outperforms Full-Trajectory ReAct, improving answer accuracy by up to 25.6 percentage points (pp); the average maximum per-step reasoning context of Full-Trajectory ReAct is $1.27$--$1.51\times$ that of ReTree. These results establish ReTree as an effective self-correcting memory abstraction for long-horizon search.

</details>


### [163] [Ex-Omni-2D: Expressive Omni-Modal Dialogue Models with Native Visual Presence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10720)
*Haoyu Zhang,Zhipeng Li,Xiaoying Tang,Tianshu Yu,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ex-Omni-2D框架，通过生成包含文本、个性化语音和参考条件视频的协调响应，实现了全模态对话模型的视觉具身化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全模态对话模型能够理解多模态输入并合成语音回复，但其响应仍然缺乏视觉具身性，无法生成配套的视觉内容。

Method: 提出Visual Thought Plan（VTP）结构化预测场景、情感和运动；使用多码本语音单元作为共享的声学-时间接口实现语音与视频帧的在线对齐；采用Teacher-Student蒸馏架构，将全序列视频生成器蒸馏为流式学生模型，引入Prefix Streaming机制减少累积退化。

Result: 在四步推理下，完整的四GPU流水线在400×720/720×400分辨率下达到端到端RTF为1.293的实时性能，提供了实用的质量-效率平衡点。

Conclusion: Ex-Omni-2D成功实现了视觉具身化的全模态对话生成，通过共享接口设计避免了大规模查询-文本-语音-视频监督数据的需求，并实现了实时推理能力。

Abstract: Omni-modal dialogue models can understand multimodal inputs and synthesize spoken replies, yet their responses remain visually disembodied. We introduce \textbf{Ex-Omni-2D}, an omni-modal dialogue framework that generates a coordinated response comprising text, personalized speech, and reference-conditioned video. Given a multimodal query, reference image, and reference audio, the model predicts a structured \textit{Visual Thought Plan} (VTP) describing scene, emotion, and motion, followed by response text and native multi-codebook speech units. These units form a shared acoustic-temporal interface: they are decoded into speech and aligned online with video frames. This interface enables the response and avatar pathways to be learned from heterogeneous speech, dialogue, and avatar-video data, avoiding the need for large-scale query--text--speech--video supervision. A full-sequence Video Generator serves as the primary Teacher. For efficient incremental generation, we further distill it into a few-step block-causal \emph{Streaming Student} whose Prefix Streaming mechanism carries a clean latent across consecutive chunks to reduce cumulative late-chunk degradation. With four-step inference, the complete four-GPU pipeline achieves an end-to-end RTF of 1.293 at $400\times720$/$720\times400$, providing a practical quality--efficiency operating point.

</details>


### [164] [Tree-of-Ideas: Automated Research Ideation via Cross-Trajectory Reasoning over Scholarly Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10740)
*Xun Li,Yiying Yang,Pengtao Li,Xiao Yao,Suyu Liu,Xiaoyang Ye,Ziyu Lu,Yuan Yao,Yangning Li,Yinghui Li,Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Tree-of-Ideas (ToI)框架，通过追踪研究轨迹的演化与交互，生成高质量研究想法，性能接近人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的研究想法生成需要追踪研究问题和解决方案在文献中的演化过程。现有方法将论文视为非结构化上下文或将学术演化建模为孤立的引用链，忽略了研究轨迹之间的交互作用。

Method: 提出两阶段框架Tree-of-Ideas (ToI)：1) EvoTrace从引用中重构分支学术轨迹，追踪演化的方法、已解决问题和研究空白；2) EvoAgent跨轨迹推理，识别趋同问题和互补解决方案，生成有依据的研究想法。

Result: 在六个AI研究主题上，ToI在自动方法中获得最高分（6.27分 vs. 最强基线5.36分，10分制），在新颖性（6.36）和依据性（7.00）方面表现优异，且分数接近人类论文参考水平（6.29）。

Conclusion: 跨路径演化推理能够有效提升研究想法生成的质量，证明了建模研究轨迹交互的重要性。

Abstract: Effective research ideation requires moving beyond a static understanding of prior work to trace how research problems and solutions evolve across the literature. Existing methods either treat papers as unstructured context or model scholarly evolution as isolated citation chains, overlooking interactions among research trajectories. We propose Tree-of-Ideas (ToI), a two-stage framework. EvoTrace reconstructs branching scholarly trajectories from citations, tracking evolving methods, resolved problems, and gaps. EvoAgent then reasons across trajectories to identify convergent problems and complementary solutions, generating grounded research ideas. Across six AI research topics, ToI achieves the highest score among automatic methods (6.27 vs. 5.36 for the strongest baseline on a 10-point scale), with strong Novelty (6.36) and Groundedness (7.00). Also, its score approaches that of human-paper references (6.29), demonstrating the value of cross-path evolutionary reasoning.

</details>


### [165] [Compositional Benchmark Synthesis for Hierarchical Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10765)
*Farnaz Soleimani,Abdelghani Chibani,Yacine Amirat,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个从扁平化单标签动作语料库合成四层语义层次（动作、活动、低层意图和高层意图）基准数据集的生成与评估框架，并通过解耦生成规则与评估规则来避免循环监督风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模语料库仅提供孤立的原子级标注片段，缺乏时间组合结构，而录制的复合活动语料库则提供浅层、领域狭窄且固定的层次结构，难以满足从原子动作到长期意图的跨抽象层次人类行为识别需求。

Method: 提出了一个基准生成框架，利用过渡模型在保持主体一致性约束下组装片段，采用覆盖感知采样器降低主体使用基尼系数；通过将序列生成规则与评估中使用的一阶逻辑规则解耦，解决循环监督风险问题。

Result: 生成了15,002个片段，四种不同模型家族的基线均出现0.13-0.17 macro-F1的组合式留出差距，包括最佳识别效果的图感知模型；覆盖感知采样器将主体使用基尼从0.566降至0.248。

Conclusion: 组合式留出差距在所有基线中普遍存在，表明这是基准的结构性特征而非模型伪影，验证了基准设计的有效性；该方法成功解决了数据集生成中的循环监督风险问题。

Abstract: Recognizing human behavior across levels of abstraction, from atomic actions to long-horizon intentions, requires data annotated along a semantic hierarchy. Large corpora provide isolated, atomically labeled clips without temporal composition, whereas recorded composite-activity corpora offer shallow, domain-narrow, fixedhierarchies. A benchmark-generation and evaluation frameworkis proposed that synthesizes a four-level hierarchical-intention benchmark, spanning actions, activities, low-level intentions (LLIs), and high-level intentions (HLIs), from a flat single-label action corpus while retaining real pre-extracted features at the action level. Episodes are assembled by a transition model under a subject-consistency constraint, and a coverage-aware sampler reduces the subject usage Gini from 0.566 to 0.248. Synthesizing such a benchmark raises a circular-supervision risk that recorded datasets avoid: if the rules generating the episodes also govern the evaluation, models can succeed by recovering the generator rather than through genuine reasoning. Validity is addressed by design, holding sequence-generation rules disjoint from the first-order-logic rules used at evaluation. The instantiation yields 15,002 episodes. Four reference baselines from different model families characterize difficulty, not as recognition methods. A compositional held-out gap of 0.13 to 0.17 macro-F1 appears across all baselines, including a graph-aware model that recognizes best yet does not close the gap, indicating a structural property of the benchmark rather than a model artifact. A logic-free baseline still violates the held-out semantic rules above their intrinsic data rate, and the order-destroying control changes macro-F1 within seed variation, serving as a generator-consistency check. Theontology, transition model, and generator are released so the benchmark can beregenerated and extended.

</details>


### [166] [Rule of Thumb: Explaining Artificial Intelligence Systems using Partial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10766)
*Kaivalya Rawal,Daria Onitiu,Brent Mittelstadt,Sandra Wachter,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Rule of Thumb"(RoT)解释方法，通过识别最相关特征来解释AI系统决策，适用于零样本分类、系统审计和科学发现，满足AI监管要求且计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释人工智能(XAI)需要有效解释AI系统的决策过程。现有方法在零样本分类、黑盒系统审计等场景下存在局限性，且计算效率较低。需要一种模型无关、符合监管要求、计算高效的解释方法。

Method: 提出"Rule of Thumb"(RoT)解释方法，采用新颖的公式化方法识别预测特定数据点AI系统行为的最相关特征。该方法不依赖模型内部访问，属于模型无关方法(model-agnostic)，提供熟悉的界面和可视化。

Result: RoT方法成功应用于三个场景：(a)大语言模型(LLMs)的零样本分类；(b)无模型访问权限的黑盒AI系统审计；(c)AI科学发现应用。相比替代方案，RoT计算速度显著更快。该方法满足领先AI监管法规要求。

Conclusion: RoT为XAI提供了一种新颖、高效、合规的解决方案，具有广泛适用性和实际应用价值，为可解释AI领域贡献了实用的工具和方法。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) seeks to explain how an Artificial Intelligence (AI) system arrived at a particular decision. We propose ''Rule of Thumb'' (RoT) explanations, a new approach to XAI based upon a novel formulation that identifies the most relevant features for predicting the behaviour of an AI system, for a particular datapoint. We show how RoT is well-suited to enable XAI in: (a) zero-shot classification using large language models (LLMs), (b) auditing of opaque AI systems without model access, and (c) the use of AI in scientific discovery. Additionally, RoT meets specific requirements from leading AI regulations, provides a familiar interface and visualisations for XAI practitioners, is model-agnostic, and is substantially faster than alternatives.
  Code available at: https://github.com/KaiRawal/Rule-of-Thumb-Explaining-Artificial-Intelligence-Systems-using-Partial-Information

</details>


### [167] [SkillLens: Visual Skill Cards for Retrieval-Augmented GUI Action Prediction and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10775)
*Zhou Liu,Ligang Huang,Zeli Su,Zewei Pan,Zhaoyang Han,Xing Chen,Yuanfeng Song,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Visual Skill Cards (VSCs)，一种将可复用程序与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定的状态条件记忆表示，显著提升了GUI智能体的任务完成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体虽然能感知丰富的软件界面，但其决策缺乏视觉程序记忆：它们可能识别单个控件，却无法识别当前活动的熟悉工作流、下一步重要的控件或确认进度的证据。原始交互轨迹信息丰富但冗长嘈杂，纯文本技能则往往忽略了使程序可应用的视觉状态。

Method: 提出Visual Skill Cards (VSCs)作为状态条件记忆表示，通过SkillLens框架从异构交互经验中构建VSC。在推理时，检索相关卡片并选择性扩展证据供固定的视觉-语言模型执行器进行基于接地信息的GUI动作预测。同时提出CardDistill方法，利用VSC证据作为特权教师上下文训练学生模型。

Result: 在Multimodal-Mind2Web和WebLINX-BrowserGym基准上，SkillLens使冻结的GPT-5.4-mini执行器的Step SR提升11.6分、Overall提升2.9分；CardDistill使仅学生模式的Qwen3-VL-2B分别进一步提升12.0和3.2分。

Conclusion: Visual Skill Cards成功地将视觉状态信息与可复用程序知识相结合，为GUI智能体提供了有效的视觉程序记忆机制，既能通过检索增强提升大模型性能，又能通过知识蒸馏实现轻量级部署，证明了视觉状态条件记忆表示在计算机使用智能体中的重要价值。

Abstract: Computer-using agents can perceive rich software interfaces, yet their decisions often lack visual procedural memory: they may recognize individual controls without identifying which familiar workflow is active, which control matters next, or what evidence would confirm progress. Raw interaction traces preserve such information but are long and noisy to condition on, whereas text-only skills often omit the visual state that makes a procedure applicable. We introduce Visual Skill Cards (VSCs), a state-conditioned memory representation that binds reusable procedures with applicability cues, visual evidence, and verification signals. SkillLens constructs VSCs from heterogeneous interaction experience through Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card and, at inference time, retrieves relevant cards and selectively expands only the evidence needed by a fixed visual-language model executor for grounded GUI action prediction. The same representation also supports CardDistill, which uses VSC evidence as privileged teacher context to train a student that acts without runtime card retrieval. Across Multimodal-Mind2Web and WebLINX-BrowserGym, SkillLens improves the frozen GPT-5.4-mini executor by +11.6 points in Step SR and +2.9 points in Overall, respectively; CardDistill further improves the corresponding student-only Qwen3-VL-2B metrics by +12.0 and +3.2 points.

</details>


### [168] [ChemWorld: Programmable Chemical Worlds for Controlled and Replayable Agent Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10792)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ChemWorld，这是一个可编程的化学环境，通过将可重用的过程和观测组件编译为可执行世界，为自主化学研究提供了可控、可重现的实验平台，支持系统性地研究不同化学世界中的实验行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主化学研究面临两难困境：物理实验室虽然能提供真实的物质证据，但成本高昂且难以进行精确匹配的干预实验；而大多数数字环境往往保持底层实验世界相对固定，缺乏灵活性。因此迫切需要一种既能提供可控实验条件又能灵活变化的实验环境，以便研究代理在不同化学世界中的实验行为。

Method: 研究者开发了ChemWorld可编程化学环境，其核心设计包括：(1) 将可重用的过程和观测组件编译成可执行世界；(2) 将代理可访问的公开实验契约与评估者拥有的化学和物质定律分离，使研究者能够改变单一隐藏定律而保持公开任务和交互条件固定；(3) 实现事务性执行以记录操作、失败、资源变化和状态转换，支持完整环境-行动轨迹的精确重演和审计。通过参考注册表、52个生成组合以及模块、接口、编译和无效操作测试进行了全面验证。

Result: 通过八组确定性实验案例验证了共享生命周期语义、故障恢复和精确重演能力；六组父子世界分叉对在匹配的公开条件下隔离了单一私有定律干预的效果；独立代理通过相同的公开接口在非参考世界中完成了完整生命周期。全面普查验证覆盖了参考注册表、52个生成的组合以及模块、接口、编译和无效操作测试。

Conclusion: ChemWorld成功提供了一个可控、可重演的基底，用于研究跨系统变化的化学世界的实验行为，与物理实验室证据和校准形成互补，为自主化学研究提供了重要的实验平台。

Abstract: Autonomous chemistry increasingly depends on environments in which agents can repeatedly act, observe, and adapt.Physical laboratories provide essential real-material evidence but are costly to repeat and difficult to use for tightly matched interventions, whereas most digital environments keep the underlying experimental world largely fixed. We introduce ChemWorld, a programmable chemical environment in which reusable process and observation components are compiled into executable worlds. ChemWorld separates the public experimental contract available to an agent from evaluator-owned chemical and material laws. Researchers can therefore vary world composition and operating conditions, or change a single hidden law while holding the public task and interaction conditions fixed. Transactional execution records operations, failures, resource changes, and state transitions, allowing complete environment-action trajectories to be replayed exactly and audited. Full-census qualification covered the reference registry, 52 generated compositions, and module, interface, compilation, and invalid-action tests. Eight deterministic experimental cases demonstrated shared lifecycle semantics, failure recovery, and exact replay, while six parent-child world-fork pairs isolated the effects of single private-law interventions under matched public conditions. An independent agent also completed a full lifecycle in a non-reference world through the same public interface. Within the declared component and model domain, ChemWorld provides a controlled and replayable substrate for studying experimentation across systematically varied chemical worlds, complementary to physical-laboratory evidence and calibration.

</details>


### [169] [EvoMem: Memory-Augmented Evolution for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10795)
*Viktor Volkov,Valentin Khrulkov,Andrey V. Galichin,Danil Sivtsov,Nikita Glazkov,Olga Volkova,Konstantin Pchelin,Iaroslav Bespalov,Dmitry V. Dylov,Petr Anokhin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoMem，一种面向LLM演化式程序搜索的持久记忆架构，通过将成功的变异策略转化为结构化建议并在后续运行中检索复用，减少重复探索，在多数任务上实现性能或速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的演化式程序搜索框架通常在单次运行后丢弃成功的变异知识，导致重复发现相似思路，缺乏跨运行与跨任务的学习与迁移能力，限制了搜索效率与策略复用。

Method: 提出EvoMem持久记忆架构，分为两个阶段：（1）运行后从成功变异事件中提取并存储带有来源的任务相关建议；（2）在后续演化过程中，根据当前任务与程序上下文检索少量相关指令以指导变异。该方法将变异知识结构化保存并在后续运行中检索复用。

Result: 在几何优化、多跳问答、GPU核优化及相关基准上，大多数设置下目标指标或搜索速度有正向平均提升，但存在任务间差异；整体显示持久记忆有助于减少部分冗余探索并提升策略复用与适应。

Conclusion: EvoMem验证了在LLM驱动的演化式程序搜索中引入持久记忆的可行性，能够减少重复探索并提高成功策略的复用与迁移，为进一步的跨运行与跨任务学习研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Successful mutation strategies in evolutionary code search may contain reusable knowledge that is useful beyond a single run, and in some cases may transfer across related tasks and domains. However, existing LLM-driven evolutionary frameworks largely discard such knowledge, repeatedly rediscovering similar ideas and limiting opportunities for cross-run and cross-task learning. We introduce EvoMem, a persistent memory architecture for LLM-based evolutionary program search that captures and reuses candidate mutation knowledge. EvoMem converts successful mutation events into structured, task-aware advice for future runs. It operates in two phases: after each run, it extracts and stores promising ideas with provenance, and during subsequent evolution, it retrieves a small set of relevant instructions based on the current task and program context to guide mutation. Across geometric optimization, multi-hop question answering, GPU kernel optimization, and related benchmarks, our experiments show positive average improvements in target metrics or search speed for most evaluated settings, while also revealing variability across tasks. Overall, EvoMem provides evidence that persistent memory can reduce some redundant exploration and improve the reuse and adaptation of successful strategies in LLM-driven evolutionary search.

</details>


### [170] [Hypothesis Frontier: Verifier Guided LLM and Symbolic Search for First-Order Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10843)
*Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hypothesis Frontier框架，通过验证器引导的神经符号方法，结合LLM生成和符号推理来求解一阶概念合成问题，显著提高了问题求解成功率并简化了结果公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶概念合成需要从有限的多个关系结构中推断出一个一致的分类公式，但量词化的一阶公式搜索空间巨大，且LLM生成的公式往往语义上有希望但并不完全正确，需要有效的验证和修正机制。

Method: 提出Hypothesis Frontier框架：(1)对每个LLM生成的公式在所有训练对象上进行验证；(2)保留最强的已验证假设作为锚点；(3)利用剩余错误指导后续生成；(4)使用符号处理修复无效公式并简化训练有效的公式。

Result: 在相同的模型、问题集和LLM轮次预算下，Hypothesis Frontier相比重复原始提示生成解决了明显更多的问题；最终公式选择后，精确简化缩短了许多训练有效的公式。

Conclusion: 精确的符号推理既有助于解决更多的归纳问题，也能压缩许多结果公式，证明了神经符号方法结合LLM和符号推理的有效性。

Abstract: First-order concept synthesis asks a system to infer one formula that classifies labeled objects consistently across several finite relational structures. Every candidate can be evaluated exactly, but quantified first-order formulas form a vast search space, and LLM outputs are often semantically promising without being fully correct. We introduce Hypothesis Frontier, a verifier-guided neurosymbolic framework that evaluates each LLM formula on every training object, retains the strongest verified hypothesis across rounds, and uses its remaining errors to guide subsequent generation. Symbolic processing repairs invalid formulas while remaining anchored to the LLM-generated hypothesis, and simplifies train-valid formulas without changing any training prediction. Under matched models, problem sets, and LLM-round budgets, Hypothesis Frontier solves substantially more problems than repeated original-prompt generation. After the final formulas are selected, exact simplification shortens many train-valid formulas while preserving every training prediction. Exact symbolic reasoning therefore helps both to solve more induction problems and to compress many of the resulting formulas.

</details>


### [171] [Enhanced Filtering Algorithms for the Euclidean Traveling Salesperson Problem and its variants in Constraint Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10881)
*Alessandro Bertagnon,Marco Gavanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了新的欧几里得TSP过滤算法，通过利用几何信息在CLP框架下实现了比现有方法更强的约束传播，并能扩展到EGTSP等变体问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的约束规划（CP）文献通常计算完整的距离矩阵来求解欧几里得TSP，将其视为一般TSP问题处理，但这种方法忽略并浪费了节点坐标本身携带的几何信息，导致约束传播能力受限。

Method: 在约束逻辑编程（CLP）框架下，设计并实现了新的过滤算法，这些算法直接利用节点的欧几里得坐标和几何信息来增强约束传播能力。同时，该方法被扩展到其他欧几里得变体，包括欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP）。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统距离矩阵方法相比，本文提出的基于几何信息的算法在计算效率上具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用欧几里得空间中的几何信息，可以设计出比传统距离矩阵方法更高效的约束传播算法，这对智能车辆、智能交通系统及物流路径规划等实际应用具有重要的理论和实践价值。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) is one of the best-known problems in computer science and arises in many engineering applications, such as smart vehicles and intelligent transportation systems. In the "Euclidean" case, each node is defined by its coordinates in the plane and distances are computed using the Euclidean metric. In the Constraint Programming (CP) literature, the Euclidean TSP is typically addressed by computing the full distance matrix and treating it as a general case; however this approach ignores the geometric information carried by the points' coordinates. In this work, we propose new filtering algorithms, implemented in Constraint Logic Programming (CLP), that exploit such geometric information to achieve stronger constraint propagation than existing approaches. Moreover, we show how this methodology can be extended to other Euclidean variants of the TSP, including the Euclidean Generalized Traveling Salesperson Problem (EGTSP), which is relevant in practical routing and logistics applications. Experimental results demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [172] [ComBodied Agents: a New Paradigm of Human-Centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10915)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Zhibin Wang,Feixiang Wang,Kelong Mao,Hao Sun,Zhiyao Luo,Jiankai Tang,Lei Li,Jiadong Guo,Minheng Ni,Weicong Lin,Chenxi Yang,Hongxiang Gao,Zhenghua Chen,Yang Bai,Min Wu,Jun Cheng,Huazhu Fu,Dacheng Tao,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“Combodied Agents”新范式，将软件代理和实体代理整合为以人为中心的闭环系统，持续感知、建模和干预个人状态轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能代理存在结构性缺陷：数字代理只改变软件状态，实体代理只改变物理状态，都无法解释人的真实状态（如忘记、困惑、副作用或拒绝服药），也不清楚何种支持合适，缺乏对人的状态和自主性的建模与干预。

Method: 提出Combodied Agents框架，通过多模态感知重建事件、可纠正的纵向记忆提供上下文、个人世界模型预测未来状态，并在同意、安全等约束下选择干预策略，形成闭环；采用目的受限、不确定感知、用户可纠正的表示，而非完备的人体数字孪生。

Result: 统一了个人助理、健康代理等碎片化能力；组织了设计空间，提出场景中心评估、自主性保持指标、基准要求、边缘原生个人模型和治理方向，推动代理AI从任务完成转向持续人本福祉。

Conclusion: Combodied Agents填补了Agentic AI的结构性空白，将人的演变状态与自主性作为核心，整合软硬件与人工服务为行动通道，实现长期、合规、以人为本的智能支持。

Abstract: After an older adult misses a medication dose, a software agent can send another reminder and an embodied agent can bring the medication. Yet neither explains whether the person forgot, is confused, has side effects, or deliberately refused, nor what support is appropriate. This reveals a structural gap in Agentic AI: Digital Agents primarily transform software states, while Embodied Agents transform physical states; neither makes a person's evolving state and agency the primary object of modeling, intervention, and evaluation. We introduce Combodied Agents, a human-centered paradigm that perceives, models, predicts, and supports individual human-state trajectories over time, using software tools, sensors, wearables, robots, and human services as action channels rather than end goals. We unify fragmented capabilities across personal assistants, health agents, AI companions, and adaptive human--AI systems into a closed loop: event-based multimodal perception reconstructs meaningful personal events; longitudinal, correctable memory provides temporal context; Personal World Models estimate future personal states and outcomes under alternative decisions and interventions; and an admissible intervention policy selects proportionate support under consent, uncertainty, safety, reversibility, and user control. Feedback from the person and environment updates the loop. Rather than requiring an exhaustive Human Digital Twin, the framework uses purpose-bounded, uncertainty-aware, user-correctable representations. We organize the design space by human-state targets, relational contexts, and agent roles, and propose scenario-centered evaluation, agency-preservation metrics, benchmark requirements, edge-native personal models, and governance directions. Combodied Agents shift Agentic AI from external task completion toward sustained human benefit.

</details>


### [173] [IO Factory: Simulating AI-Enabled Influence Campaigns at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10920)
*Lukasz Olejnik,Wenchao Dong,Jonas R. Kunst,Signe Riemer-Sørensen,Tobias Herb,Meeyoung Cha,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IO Factory，一个AI驱动的框架，用于模拟信息和影响力活动为完全集成、可追溯的过程，解决了AI群体协调行动的检测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字操控威胁已从单个语言模型生成的说服性文本扩展到AI群体（即持久的协调代理组），它们能适应平台反馈并将有组织的活动伪装成普通社交互动。由于此类活动无法仅从孤立消息中识别，必须在整个连续过程中进行分析。

Method: IO Factory在受控的模拟平台中表示影响力活动的完整过程，连接参与者角色、平台行动、曝光记录、结构化的模型评估以及模拟人群中的配置变化。系统记录每次运行中使用的参与者、目标、行动约束、曝光路径和测量规则。

Result: 在多达100,000个代理的配置上实施并评估了该架构，证明IO Factory能够大规模执行活动时间线，并生成可检查的曝光证据和配置信念变量的测量变化。

Conclusion: 通过记录完整的活动要素，IO Factory支持对协调影响力进行可重现研究和红队分析，为应对AI群体的数字操控威胁提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We introduce IO Factory, an AI-driven framework for simulating information and influence campaigns as fully integrated, traceable processes. The threat of digital manipulation now extends beyond persuasive text from individual language models to AI swarms, i.e., persistent groups of coordinated agents that adapt to platform feedback and disguise organized campaigns as ordinary social interaction. Because such campaigns cannot be identified from isolated messages alone, they must be analyzed across a continuous spectrum of planning, platform action, exposure, interpretation, measurement, and adaptation. IO Factory represents this process inside a controlled simulated platform, linking actor roles, platform actions, exposure records, structured model-based evaluations, and configured changes in the simulated population. We implement the architecture and evaluate it across configurations of up to 100,000 agents. The results show that IO Factory executes campaign timelines at scale and produces inspectable evidence of exposure and measured movement in configured belief variables. By recording the actors, objectives, action constraints, exposure paths, and measurement rules used in each run, IO Factory supports reproducible research and red-team analysis of coordinated influence.

</details>


### [174] [ThinkRetrieve: Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning Traces for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10928)
*Vaibhav Singh,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Sarvesh Gharat,Soumyabrata Pal,Ramasuri Narayanam,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大型推理模型顺序测试时扩展效果递减的问题，提出ThinkRetrieve框架，通过在推理步骤中动态检索已解决问题的示例来增强推理轨迹，在多项推理任务上实现了最高60%的相对准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过增加推理时计算来生成长链式思维推理，但研究发现顺序测试时扩展往往产生递减甚至负回报，因为更长的推理轨迹会增加不确定性、错误累积和偏离原始问题的风险。

Method: 提出ThinkRetrieve测试时扩展框架，利用外部问题-解决方案语料库，在推理的每个中间步骤动态检索相关的已解决示例，并将其直接注入到思考轨迹中，为模型提供推理方法的指导而不仅仅是相关事实。

Result: 在五个推理模型（1.5B-8B参数）上，针对GSM-8K、MATH-500、AIME 2025和SciQ数据集进行实验，ThinkRetrieve在标准测试时扩展基础上持续提高准确率，在AIME 2025上达到最高60%的相对增益。

Conclusion: ThinkRetrieve框架成功解决了传统顺序测试时扩展的局限性，通过动态检索增强推理过程，显著提升了大型推理模型在复杂推理任务上的性能表现。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by allocating additional inference-time compute to generate extended chain-of-thought reasoning. However, recent studies reveal that sequential test-time scaling often yields diminishing or even negative returns, as longer traces exhibit increased uncertainty, error compounding, and drift from the original problem. We propose ThinkRetrieve, a test-time scaling framework that augments the reasoning traces of LRMs with dynamically retrieved solved examples at each reasoning step. Given an external corpus of problems paired with step-by-step solutions, ThinkRetrieve retrieves relevant exemplars at each intermediate step and injects them directly into the thinking trace, providing the model with guidance on how to reason rather than merely what facts are relevant. Experiments across five reasoning models (1.5B--8B parameters) on GSM-8K, MATH-500, AIME 2025, and SciQ demonstrate that ThinkRetrieve consistently improves accuracy over standard test-time scaling, with relative gains of up to $60\%$ on AIME 2025.

</details>


### [175] [FedCGR: Federated Cross-Domain Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10929)
*Zhuodong Liu,Hugen Lv,Xiangyu Li,Bohan Guo,Peiyu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FedCGR，一种联邦跨域生成推荐框架，通过语义ID序列实现跨域对齐，在保护隐私的同时实现了优异的推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦跨域推荐中跨域对齐困难，因为跨客户端的行为锚点（如重叠用户和共享交互信号）通常稀疏、不可用或隐私敏感。现有方法难以在不交换私有交互的情况下有效传递偏好知识。

Method: 将物品表示为从公共元数据导出的离散语义ID（SID）序列，通过共享词汇表而非交换私有交互来诱导跨域物品对齐；提出FedCGR框架，通过可靠性感知语义接口注入本地协同过滤证据，训练原型个性化生成器，根据域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，同时保持域特定参数本地化。

Result: 在六个亚马逊跨域场景上的实验表明，FedCGR持续优于联邦生成基线，在全排序和采样评估协议下与强序列和联邦跨域推荐方法达到竞争性性能。

Conclusion: FedCGR成功解决了联邦跨域推荐中的对齐难题，在保持物品语言稳定性和显式适应的同时实现了有效的知识迁移，为隐私保护下的跨域推荐提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) transfers preference knowledge across related domains, but federated deployment makes cross-domain alignment difficult because the behavioral anchors that align item spaces, such as overlapping users and shared interaction signals, are often sparse, unavailable, or privacy-sensitive across clients. To address this tension, we revisit federated CDR as generation over a stable semantic item language. By representing items as discrete semantic ID (SID) sequences derived from public item-side metadata, cross-domain item alignment is induced by a shared vocabulary rather than by exchanging private interactions or aligning domain-specific embeddings. Directly federating SID-based generators, however, introduces two design constraints: the SID tokenizer must remain fixed to preserve cross-client token consistency, which creates a semantic-only bottleneck because local collaborative filtering (CF) signals cannot be globally shared or aligned; meanwhile, standard federated averaging can cause negative transfer under domain heterogeneity. To overcome these constraints, we propose FedCGR, a federated generative CDR framework that keeps the item language stable and makes adaptation explicit. FedCGR injects local CF evidence through a reliability-aware semantic interface and trains a prototype-personalized generator that selectively aggregates shared parameters according to domain relatedness while keeping domain-specific quantities local. Experiments on six Amazon cross-domain scenarios show that FedCGR consistently outperforms federated generative baselines and achieves competitive performance against strong sequential and federated CDR methods under both full-ranking and sampled evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [176] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team,XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出XCoT-VLA框架，通过用紧凑的可执行CoT令牌替换冗长的自然语言推理，实现了自动驾驶中的实时推理与轨迹生成，显著降低了推理开销并提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型使用冗长的自然语言链式推理(CoT)进行场景理解和语义推理，但这种方式不适合实时控制：它是开放式的、解码成本高、难以作为面向动作的表示进行优化。

Method: 提出XCoT-VLA框架：1) 用自动构建的Reason-Action监督学习紧凑的可执行CoT令牌替代描述性推理；2) XCoT序列保持在上下文中，通过共享多模态自注意力条件化固定轨迹查询；3) 确定性令牌-功能路由将Reason FFN应用于XCoT令牌，Control FFN应用于轨迹查询进行流匹配轨迹生成；4) 引入XCoT策略优化(XCPO)作为可选的精化扩展。

Result: 仅使用2-6个可执行XCoT令牌表示驾驶导向推理，大幅减少自回归推理开销并保持在实时规划预算内。在通用分布集上纵向ADE从1.645降至1.323，在变道场景中横向FDE从1.616降至0.648。

Conclusion: 驾驶导向推理可以是紧凑的、可执行的，并能直接连接到轨迹生成，证明了可执行令牌表示在自动驾驶VLA模型中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>


### [177] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了V-FiLLM框架，用于自动生成基于真实表格的金融推理基准测试，揭示了现有LLM在复杂金融推理上的不足，并证明了低成本微调的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试在STEM领域取得了显著进展，但基于结构化数据的金融推理研究相对不足。现有方法存在标注成本高、可能继承生成模型错误率等问题，缺乏可扩展且准确的评估基准。

Method: 提出了V-FiLLM框架，从可执行的计算树生成金融推理基准。该方法通过符号评估计算树获取真值，并将其渲染为自然语言问题，完全脱离模型标注循环。框架包含四个独立可控的难度轴：计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度和上下文大小，支持任意规模的无成本生成。

Result: 在开源模型上的评估显示：随着推理深度增加，准确率下降高达51%；在对抗性数值扰动下准确率下降47个百分点。通过轻量级LoRA微调验证的思维链，在保留问题上的准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，在FinQA上比基础模型提升5个百分点。

Conclusion: V-FiLLM框架能够低成本、大规模生成准确的金融推理基准，揭示了LLM在表格金融推理方面的鲁棒性挑战。针对性的低成本适配是金融问答中组合推理的有前景方向。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [178] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SkillZip，一种无需评估的技能压缩方法，通过寻找最短的忠实结构解释来压缩自演化智能体积累的冗余技能，有效解决技能注入成本高和维护困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化智能体通过追加成功过程和失败修复来积累可重用技能，导致相同需求在多个分支、示例和警告中重复陈述，常见动作序列被复制而非重用，使得技能注入成本高昂且难以维护。现有的通用提示压缩方法不适用于这种场景，因为技能包含名称、描述、工作流、工具约束等复杂结构，而评估引导的压缩方法会引入成本和评估集依赖问题。

Method: 提出SkillZip方法，采用“解释一次，引用多次”的直觉，在适用范围内只陈述一次重复规则，将重复动作序列分解为共享过程，仅保留差异作为显式异常。形式化为技能契约和残差上的类型化最小描述长度目标，对每个提取的触发器、工作流边、工具需求、义务和输出字段施加硬覆盖约束。支持一次性模式和持续的Zip-on-Write模式，无需重放任务或重新解析完整历史。

Result: 通过全面的实验评估，证明了SkillZip在压缩性能、泛化能力和成本开销方面的有效性和优越性，能够在不损失功能的前提下有效压缩冗余技能。

Conclusion: SkillZip成功解决了自演化智能体技能冗余问题，提供了一种高效、无需评估的技能压缩方案，支持增量更新，在保持功能完整性的同时显著降低了技能的复杂度和维护成本。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [179] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了轨道交通站点知识图谱(RTSKG)数据集，显式建模城市实体间的空间和语义交互关系，支持城市级轨道交通站点相关任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级轨道交通站点相关任务(如客流预测)需要大规模城市数据，但现有研究在数据组织方面往往忽视了各种城市实体间的复杂交互关系。

Method: 构建轨道交通站点知识图谱(RTSKG)数据集，采用专门设计的统一模式整合异构城市实体(轨道交通站点、道路段、兴趣点等)，显式建模不同类型城市实体间的空间和语义交互关系，并以关联数据形式发布。

Result: 在站点周边商店推荐和知识增强客流预测任务上的评估证明了RTSKG的有效性，验证了其支持城市级轨道交通站点分析的潜力。

Conclusion: RTSKG数据集通过显式建模城市实体间的复杂交互关系，为城市级轨道交通站点相关任务提供了有效的数据支持，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [180] [Why Does CLAUDE.md Keep Growing? Catastrophic Remembering in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11095)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现代理编码README文件会无限制增长（平均增长226%），提出“灾难性记忆”现象，并通过引入提示注释有效抑制了99.3%的冗余指令增长，同时提升了真实世界代理指令遵循性能达23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际代码仓库中，代理编码的README文件（如CLAUDE.md）会无限增长，研究者将此现象追溯为“不完美回忆”：添加指令成本很低，但一旦指令的原理被遗忘，删除它而不冒着正确性回归风险的成本是指数级的O(2^|D|)。研究者将这种结果分歧命名为“灾难性记忆”，这是持续学习所围绕的“灾难性遗忘”的逆过程。为了解决这一问题，研究者受到了“如果英语是新的代码，为什么还没有注释”这一启发，尝试引入提示注释来解决这个问题。

Method: 首先，研究者在1,867个仓库的247,694条指令生命周期中刻画了这一现象，量化分析了代理提示的增长模式。然后，研究者提出通过提示注释来停止增长：通过反转IFEval获得可验证的世界，其最优提示已知，并在其中编码潜在推理的注释。最后，将同样的反转变换应用于WildIFEval，验证提示注释在真实世界代理指令遵循任务中的效果。

Result: 实验结果表明：代理提示无限制增长，在其生命周期内增长超过三倍（+226%），每次提交平均增加+4.9条净指令；指令越老，被删除的可能性越低（对数风险率为-0.032/提交）。通过提示注释，在IFEval反演实验中删除了99.3%的冗余指令（从+211.3%降至+1.4%）。在WildIFEval应用中，提示注释可将真实世界代理指令遵循性能提升高达23.1%。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统刻画了“灾难性记忆”现象，揭示了代理编码提示无限制增长的根本原因。通过引入提示注释机制，成功抑制了99.3%的冗余指令增长，并显著提升了代理指令遵循性能。研究提出“如果英语是新的代码，为什么还没有注释”的思考，为未来代理编码系统的发展提供了新的视角和解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic coding READMEs like CLAUDE.md grow without bound in real repositories, stopping only when the repository retires or someone rewrites the file wholesale. We trace this to imperfect recall: appending an instruction is always cheap, but once an instruction's rationale is gone, deleting it without risking a correctness regression costs O(2^|D|) in a prompt of |D| instructions. We name the resulting divergence catastrophic remembering, the inverse of catastrophic forgetting around which continual learning is organized. First, we characterize this phenomenon across 247,694 instruction lifetimes in 1,867 repositories: agentic prompts grow without bound, more than tripling over their lifetime (+226%), gaining +4.9 net instructions every commit; further, the older an instruction gets, the less likely it is to be deleted (log-hazard -0.032/commit). Then, we show that prompt comments can halt the growth: inverting IFEval yields verifiable worlds whose optimal prompts are known, and there comments encoding latent reasoning remove 99.3% of excess instructions (+211.3% to +1.4%). Finally, applying the same inversion to WildIFEval, we show that prompt comments can improve real-world agentic instruction-following by up to 23.1%. If English is the new code, why don't we have comments yet?

</details>


### [181] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出sLTN扩展框架，在逻辑张量网络中引入结构维度作为一等公民，使其能直接表达时序、序列和图结构约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LTN框架主要适用于扁平化个体集合，无法显式捕获时序顺序、序列位置或图连接等结构化组织，限制了其在结构化数据上的表达能力。

Method: 将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为命名张量轴引入语言，使其可被显式量化、通过结构关系关联，并直接在逻辑层面表达时序、序列和关系约束；提供完整的形式化语法和模糊张量语义，并实现基于PyTorch的声明式框架。

Result: 证明了无结构维度时框架可恢复原始LTN语义作为特例；通过时序和序列推理示例验证了框架的有效性；提供了开源sltn库。

Conclusion: sLTN成功扩展了LTN的表达能力，使其能够自然处理结构化维度，同时保持与原始框架的兼容性，为神经符号计算在结构化数据上的应用提供了新工具。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


### [182] [Long-Horizon AI Research for Grothendieck Constant: A Case Study in Human-AI Mathematical Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11195)
*Alan Li,Rahul Saha,Anton Xue,Swarat Chaudhuri,Adam Klivans,Pravesh K Kothari,Raghu Meka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文展示了利用AI研究系统改进Grothendieck常数$K_G$界限的成功案例，将最佳已知界限收紧至$\frac{6π}{11} \le K_G \le \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}$，并分享了AI在数学研究中的使用经验与最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在数学研究中的应用日益增加，但如何有效使用它们往往不明确。作者希望通过改进Grothendieck常数界限的具体案例，探索AI在数学研究中的有效使用方法，为社区提供实践经验。

Method: 作者进行了详尽的案例研究，使用AI研究系统改进Grothendieck常数$K_G$的上下界，并通过创造理想条件使AI能够产生被领域专家认为新颖的见解。

Result: 成功将Grothendieck常数$K_G$的最佳已知界限收紧至$\frac{6π}{11} \le K_G \le \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}$，AI系统能够产生被领域专家认为新颖的见解。

Conclusion: 作者详细讨论了在数学研究中使用AI的经验，包括其优势与劣势，以及创造理想条件让AI产生突破性见解的方法，为AI辅助数学研究提供了实践指导。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used in mathematics research, but it is often unclear how to use them effectively. Towards this, we present an extensive case study of how AI was used to improve bounds on the Grothendieck constant $K_G$, which captures the hardness between combinatorial problems and their continuous relaxations. Specifically, while the precise value of $K_G$ is not known, we recently tightened the best known bounds to \[
  \frac{6π}{11}
  \;\le\;
  K_G
  \;\le\;
  \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}. \] Crucially, these improvements were achieved using an AI research system that could arrive at insights deemed novel by domain experts. We give a detailed discussion of our experience using AI for mathematics research, particularly touching upon its strengths and weaknesses, as well as our experience with creating ideal conditions for AI to arrive at breakthrough insights.

</details>


### [183] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六个前沿大语言模型在三种行为引导场景下的可操控性差异，发现模型不仅在引导敏感度上存在差异，其响应模式本身也各不相同，并通过线性探测定位了Llama内部的引导决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型在训练数据、目标和安全流程上各不相同，但这些差异是否会在显式引导压力下产生可测量的行为差异尚未被充分探索。现有研究缺乏对不同模型行为可操控性的系统比较，因此需要统一评估框架来揭示模型间的行为差异。

Method: 研究构建了包含300组基础-引导配对项的测试集，涵盖三类场景：价值观冲突、推理诱导和推理抑制（另含40个验证项）。六个来自不同开发者的前沿模型作为盲审评委，基于固定行为规则对所有响应进行分类，共产生24,480个判断。采用留一共识法对判断进行评分，并通过token预算补救和假设盲审提示进行控制实验。针对Llama开放权重模型，使用线性探针从残差流中解码行为，并在生成过程中注入该方向进行干预。

Result: 研究发现模型不仅在引导敏感度上存在差异，其响应模式本身也各不相同，某些响应模式仅出现在一两个模型中。GPT-5通过保留答案但转移推理披露请求表现出独特行为（99% vs 其他模型0%）。Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵制显式抑制指令。对于Llama，线性探针在留出数据上达到0.87准确率解码行为，在生成过程中注入该方向可将行为发生率从0%提升至86%。所有发现在两种控制条件下均保持稳健。

Conclusion: 前沿模型在行为可操控性上存在显著且系统的差异，不仅表现为引导敏感度不同，更体现在响应模式的质变差异上。这些行为差异可通过模型内部表征进行定位和操控，表明模型架构和训练过程的差异会在行为层面产生可测量的分化，为理解模型行为的内部机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models). Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5 resist explicit suppression instructions and in different ways. Using Llama as the open-weight model, we trace the largest behavioral split to its internals. A linear probe decodes the behavior from the residual stream at 0.87 held-out accuracy while injecting that direction during generation drives the behavior from 0% to 86% across an intervention sweep. Every finding holds under both a token-budget remediation and a control experiment with a hypothesis-blind judgment prompt.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [MemSpec: Memory-Aware Runtime for Adaptive Draft Scheduling in Speculative Decoding on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10362)
*Eunjeong Kim,Yeong Jun Jeon,Myeonggyun Han*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出MemSpec框架，通过预测引导和内存感知的运行时管理，解决了边缘设备上自适应推测解码的内存限制问题，平均提升40.7%的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应推测解码方法在内存受限的边缘设备上表现不佳，主要由于草稿模型切换开销大，且存在草稿选择与内存可用性不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出MemSpec框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)轻量级预测器根据提示和生成上下文估计草稿有效性；(2)内存感知调度器通过前瞻性常驻工作集管理，将草稿选择与执行解耦，降低模型加载开销。

Result: 在Jetson Orin Nano上的实验表明，MemSpec相比最先进的基于赌博树的自适应方法，稳态生成吞吐量平均提升40.7%，且接近oracle上界。

Conclusion: MemSpec成功解决了边缘设备上自适应推测解码的内存约束挑战，通过预测引导和内存感知调度显著提升了LLM推理吞吐量。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive large language model (LLM) inference by using a lightweight draft model to speculate multiple tokens, reducing expensive target model decoding steps. Its effectiveness depends heavily on draft selection, motivating adaptive methods that exploit variation across inputs and generation stages. On memory-constrained edge devices, however, these methods often fail to improve end-to-end throughput due to the overhead of switching between draft models. We identify a key limitation in this setting: the mismatch between draft selection and draft availability under tight memory budgets. To address this challenge, we present MemSpec, a prediction-guided, memory-aware runtime for adaptive speculative decoding on edge devices. MemSpec decouples draft selection from execution through proactive resident working-set management. A lightweight predictor estimates draft effectiveness from prompt and generation context, while a memory-aware scheduler reduces reactive model loading overhead. Experiments on a Jetson Orin Nano show that MemSpec improves steady-state generation throughput by 40.7% on average over state-of-the-art bandit-based adaptive methods while closely approaching the oracle upper bound.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Spatio-Temporal Scheduling for Robust and Efficient Multi-Transmitter Wireless Power Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10391)
*Yuna Sawada,Shino Shiraki,Nobuyoshi Kikuma,Takahiro Matsuda,Takefumi Hiraguri,Kazuki Maruta,Tomotaka Kimura*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多发射机无线功率传输系统的时空调度方法，通过协调波束成形在时变信道和阴影条件下实现了鲁棒高效的功率传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的时分无线功率传输系统虽然能利用整流天线(rectenna)的非线性特性通过时间集中功率提高效率，但依赖单点高方向性传输在时变信道条件下鲁棒性不足。需要研究如何在多发射机协调系统中提高功率传输的鲁棒性和效率。

Method: 提出了一种用于协调多发射机无线功率传输系统的时空调度方案。该方法利用多个发射机增强对信道变化的鲁棒性，并通过发射机间的协调波束成形(coordinated beamforming)利用簇间干扰(inter-cluster interference)来改善功率传输性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的方案在时变信道和阴影条件下能够实现鲁棒且高效的功率传输，验证了该方法在复杂传播环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: 时空调度策略结合多发射机协调波束成形能够有效克服单发射机系统的局限性，为多用户无线功率传输系统提供了一种在动态信道条件下兼具鲁棒性和高效率的解决方案。

Abstract: In multi-user wireless power transfer (WPT), scheduling schemes that determine the allocation of transmission resources among receivers play a crucial role in improving power transfer efficiency. Scheduling can be classified into time-division (TD) and space-division (SD) schemes, with the transmission order and direction designed to control when and to whom power is delivered. TD-WPT can exploit the nonlinear characteristics of rectennas by concentrating power in time; however, a system relying on highly directional transmission from a single location reduces robustness under time-varying channel conditions. This study investigated the effectiveness of spatio-temporal scheduling in coordinated multi-transmitter WPT systems. By employing multiple transmitters, the proposed method is robust against channel variations in delivering power. Moreover, coordinated beamforming among transmitters exploits inter-cluster interference. Simulation results demonstrate the potential of the proposed scheme for robust and efficient power supply, even under shadowing conditions.

</details>


### [186] [Link-adaptive digital twin for robust physical-layer modeling in hybrid-amplified ultra-wideband optical networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10517)
*Xiaoxuan Gao,Rentao Gu,Yingchun Wang,Xinyi Liu,Junshi Gao,Yuefeng Ji*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出链路自适应数字孪生(LA-DT)方法，通过线性调制层神经网络架构与少样本微调，实现混合放大超宽带链路的精确物理层建模与GSNR估计，在35个场景中RMSE降低超52%，未见场景仅需20样本即可快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超宽带系统中信道间受激拉曼散射(ISRS)效应加剧，现有物理层建模方法在跨场景泛化能力和计算速度方面存在局限，难以实现准确可靠的广义信噪比(GSNR)估计与容量优化。

Method: 将GSNR建模分解为ASE、NLI及EDFA前信号功率三项功率预测；构建含线性调制层(LML)的三个专用数字孪生模型提升跨场景泛化；引入三个域判别器指导少样本微调以快速适应未见场景；显式考虑拉曼放大器插入损耗以增强实际部署可靠性。

Result: 在35个场景中，LA-DT将NLI、ASE和信号功率预测的RMSE降至0.151、0.111和0.113 dBm，较基线分别提升56.0%、58.4%和52.7%；平均GSNR估计RMSE为0.114 dBm(提升55.8%)。在12个未见场景中，仅需每场景20样本的少样本微调即可保持高精度，平均GSNR RMSE为0.159 dB。

Conclusion: LA-DT有效克服了现有方法在泛化性和速度上的限制，实现了跨多样链路的精确建模与鲁棒GSNR估计，具备强适应性与实用价值，为超宽带光通信系统提供了可靠的物理层建模新方案。

Abstract: Accurate physical-layer modeling is increasingly essential for reliable ultra-wideband operation and capacity optimization, especially under the intensified inter-channel stimulated Raman scattering (ISRS) effect. This paper proposes the link-adaptive digital twin (LA-DT) for hybrid-amplified ultra-wideband links to overcome the generalization and speed limitations of existing methods, achieving accurate modeling and robust generalized signal-to-noise ratio (GSNR) estimation across diverse links. First, to address EDFA heterogeneity, the GSNR modeling task is decomposed into three key power predictions: ASE, NLI, and signal powers before EDFA entry. Second, to enhance cross-scenario generalization, three dedicated DT models are developed using a novel neural architecture with linear modulation layers (LMLs). Third, for rapid adaptation to unseen scenarios with limited data, three domain discriminators guide few-shot fine-tuning of the LMLs. Fourth, the LA-DT explicitly accounts for Raman amplifier (RA) insertion loss, improving practical deployment reliability. Results across 35 scenarios show that LA-DT reduces RMSE for NLI, ASE, and signal power predictions to 0.151, 0.111, and 0.113 dBm with improvements of 56.0%, 58.4%, and 52.7% over the baseline,and achieves an average GSNR estimation RMSE of 0.114 dBm (55.8% improvement). For 12 unseen scenarios, the LA-DT maintains high accuracy through few-shot fine-tuning with only 20 samples per scenario, achieving an average GSNR RMSE of 0.159 dB and demonstrating strong adaptability and robustness.

</details>


### [187] [Benchmarking LLM-Guided Control-Plane Policies for Backend Fault Isolation in HAProxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10532)
*Aman Chauhan,Vishnu Pendyala*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: LLM负载均衡策略在活跃参数超过3B阈值时可将5xx错误减少约88%，但以尾延迟增加2.6–2.8倍与推理成本上升为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态负载均衡器（如round-robin、least-connections）无法有效处理服务降级（如返回HTTP 500）场景，仍将流量路由至故障后端直至人工干预。研究旨在探索LLM是否能替代静态路由策略，通过读取HAProxy与Prometheus遥测数据并经由受保护的HAProxy Data Plane API隔离故障节点。

Method: 在可复现基准测试环境中向约1/3异构后端注入持续性结构故障，每10秒读取遥测。系统化评测15个开源模型（涵盖0.35B–35B参数、dense/MoE/高效稀疏架构），在3–9后端规模与两种路由算法下进行共240次实验。以活跃参数、推理模式、规模等维度评估错误率、尾延迟与控制时延。

Result: 发现约3B活跃参数的能力阈值：低于阈值时常不可靠甚至劣于无策略；超过阈值后所有模型均收敛至相对静态基线约88%的5xx错误下降。阈值近似，如Gemma 4 E2B以2B活跃参数跨过，而稠密3B Granite 4.0 Micro未跨过。代价方面：流量集中使尾延迟增加2.6–2.8倍，启用推理使token开销约十倍，易超出控制间隔并损害效果。

Conclusion: 推荐的高效运行点：超阈值模型在最低成本非推理模式、置于确定性防护机制内，可在控制延迟与成本的前提下显著提升可用性。研究明确了LLM在负载均衡中的能力边界与成本权衡，为工程落地提供量化依据。

Abstract: Static load balancers cannot mitigate a backend that is degraded rather than down: round-robin and least-connections keep routing traffic to a server returning HTTP 500s until an operator intervenes. We ask whether a Large Language Model can replace the static routing policy itself, reading HAProxy and Prometheus telemetry every 10 seconds and isolating faulty servers through guardrailed calls to the HAProxy Data Plane API. On a reproducible benchmark with a persistent structural fault built into roughly one-third of a heterogeneous fleet, we sweep 15 open-weight models across five families (0.35B to 35B total parameters; dense, mixture-of-experts, and efficient-sparse architectures), reasoning modes, fleet scales of 3 to 9 backends, and two routing algorithms, totaling 240 runs. We find a capability threshold near 3B active parameters. Below it, LLM policies are typically unreliable and sometimes worse than no policy; above it, every model, regardless of architecture, saturates near an 88% reduction in client-perceived 5xx errors over the static baseline. The threshold is approximate: Gemma 4 E2B clears it with 2B active parameters, while the dense 3B Granite 4.0 Micro does not. The availability gain has costs. Draining concentrates load onto surviving servers, inflating tail latency 2.6 to 2.8 times, and enabling reasoning multiplies token spend roughly tenfold, overrunning the control interval and degrading effectiveness. The efficient operating point is a supra-threshold model in its cheapest non-reasoning mode, wrapped inside deterministic guardrails.

</details>


### [188] [An Asynchronous Triggered MAC Protocol for Underwater Acoustic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10533)
*Bingwen Huangfu,Jiani Guo,Shanshan Song,Nan Sun,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出AT-MAC协议，将UANs中的MAC设计从同步时隙范式转变为异步调度范式，利用多智能体深度强化学习实现高效协调的信道接入，并通过负载感知公平性保护机制维持网络公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TDMA-based MAC协议依赖同步固定长度时隙范式，在低且变速的UANs中带来显著的时钟同步开销，且难以提高调度灵活性。尽管有协议尝试调整时隙长度，但仍在信道利用率和调度复杂性之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出Asynchronous Triggered MAC (AT-MAC)，引入无需时间同步的触发时隙范式，将传输调度从刚性时间线解耦，支持异步、可变长度时隙。采用增强的多智能体深度强化学习处理异步交互，设计负载感知公平性保护机制通过本地监听推断网络公平状态并引导调度校正。

Result: 基于trace和机载实验验证了AT-MAC的可行性和计算实用性。广泛仿真结果表明其在各种场景和流量条件下具有优越性和适应性。

Conclusion: AT-MAC成功实现了从同步时隙到异步调度的范式转变，在不依赖时间同步的情况下实现了高效协调的信道接入和网络公平性，为UANs中的MAC设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Time Division Multiple Access (TDMA)-based Medium Access Control (MAC) protocols have proven their practicality through extensive field trials in Underwater Acoustic Networks (UANs), attributable to their hardware-agnostic and easily implementable properties. Most existing protocols rely on a synchronized and fixed-length slot paradigm to mitigate channel contention and facilitate orderly transmissions. However, this paradigm imposes significant clock synchronization overhead in UANs with low and variable sound speed and struggles to improve scheduling flexibility. Although some protocols attempt to refine this slot paradigm (adjust the slot length to improve channel reuse efficiency or scheduling frequency), they are still constrained by the trade-off between channel utilization and scheduling complexity. To this end, this paper advocates a paradigm shift in underwater MAC design, transitioning from synchronized slot to asynchronous scheduling, and realizes it through an Asynchronous Triggered MAC (AT-MAC). AT-MAC introduces a triggered slot paradigm without time synchroniza?tion, decoupling transmission scheduling from a rigid timeline and enabling asynchronous, variable-length slots to accommodate the long and diverse propagation delays. To power this slot paradigm, AT-MAC augments conventional Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning to handle asynchronous interaction, achieving efficient coordinated channel access under partial ob?servations. It further devises a load-aware fairness guard mech?anism to enable network-wide fairness status inference solely through local overhearing, thereby guiding adaptive scheduling correction to maintain fairness. Trace-based and on-board ex?periments validate the feasibility and computational practicality of AT-MAC. Extensive simulation results further demonstrate its superiority and adaptability across various scenarios and traffic conditions.

</details>


### [189] [ImpactHO: Importance-Aware KV Cache Transfer for Multi-User Edge LLM Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10545)
*Minwoo Kim,Soochang Song,Namyoon Lee,Bang Chul Jung,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对边缘LLM在用户切换时KV缓存传输带宽不足的问题，本文提出基于重要性排序的部分缓存传输策略，通过加权水填充算法优化多用户回程带宽分配，在500ms传输窗口内实现93.7%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘计算场景中，当用户在不同边缘节点间切换时，需要传输KV缓存以保证推理连续性。然而，多个用户同时切换会导致回程网络带宽饱和，无法在移动性限制的时间窗口内完成完整缓存传输，影响服务质量。

Method: 提出基于重要性排序的部分KV缓存传输框架。首先按重要性对每个用户的KV缓存条目进行排序，优先传输高价值条目。将缓存传输建模为多用户回程带宽分配优化问题，以最大化平均准确率为目标。实验发现部分缓存准确率与缓存比例呈sigmoid关系（R²>0.99），且由于重要性排序使高价值条目优先传输，准确率曲线的凹区域覆盖几乎整个缓存范围，使每时隙分配问题成为凸优化问题。推导出闭式加权重水填充解，推广了经典信息论水填充方法并支持在线调度。

Result: 在500ms传输窗口内，所提分配器实现超过93.7%的平均准确率，与完整缓存准确率上限相差小于0.5个百分点；达到全知上界（clairvoyant upper bound）的98.2-99.5%。在RULER基准测试上，sigmoid模型拟合R²>0.99，跨多个模型和上下文长度有效。

Conclusion: 通过利用KV缓存的重要性稀疏性，将token级稀疏性转化为通信节省，所提方法有效解决了边缘LLM切换场景下的带宽约束传输问题。加权水填充算法提供闭式最优解，兼具理论最优性与实际可实现性，为边缘智能系统的无缝切换提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Edge LLMs must preserve inference continuity when a user hands over between edge nodes, requiring key-value (KV) cache transfer to the target node. However, simultaneous handovers saturate the backhaul, preventing full cache delivery within the mobility-imposed transfer window. Rather than allocating bandwidth as if all cache entries were equally valuable, we order each user's KV cache by importance and transmit only its most informative fraction, turning token-level sparsity into communication savings. We cast the transfer as a multi-user backhaul allocation problem that maximizes average accuracy across users. Each user's partial-cache accuracy serves as its utility: a sigmoid that fits measurements on the RULER benchmark with $R^2>0.99$ across models and context lengths. Because importance ordering front-loads the high-value entries, the concave region of the accuracy curve spans nearly the entire cache. Our proposed allocator keeps served users within this region, making each per-slot allocation problem convex. The optimum is derived via a closed-form weighted water-filling solution that generalizes information-theoretic water-filling and enables online scheduling. The proposed allocator attains over 93.7% average accuracy in a 500ms transfer window, within 0.5pp of the full-cache ceiling, and reaches 98.2-99.5% of a clairvoyant upper bound.

</details>


### [190] [Sensing in Low-altitude Wireless Networks: Systems, Techniques, and Developments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10555)
*Zihao Tao,Yiming Zhao,Hongtao Zhao,Zijun Gong,Ying Cui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了低空无线网络（LAWN）感知技术，从系统框架、核心技术和研究趋势三个维度展开分析，提出模型与数据驱动的多模态感知方法，填补了现有感知方案与LAWN实际需求之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空空域具有高度动态性和安全关键性特征，使感知成为低空无线网络不可或缺的组成部分。然而，当前最先进的感知方案与LAWN实际感知需求之间存在显著不匹配，现有研究缺乏针对LAWN感知的系统性综述。

Method: 从系统框架、核心技术和研究趋势三个维度系统回顾LAWN感知：首先分析感知系统框架（包括概念、服务与任务、节点与目标、场景）；然后从传播介质、协作性、方法论和模态四个角度对现有感知技术进行对比分析；最后提出模型与数据驱动的多模态感知方法进行实时空中目标感知案例研究。

Result: 相比现有LAWN或感知技术综述，本文提供了更全面、更具针对性的LAWN感知专项综述。总结了可部署LAWN感知系统的未来研究方向，涵盖非协作与协作感知、模型驱动与数据驱动感知、以及模型与数据驱动多模态感知。通过案例研究验证了模型与数据驱动多模态方法在实时空中目标感知中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了LAWN感知研究的空白，为未来可部署的LAWN感知系统提供了系统性指导，推动了低空空域感知技术的发展和应用落地。

Abstract: The highly dynamic and safety-critical characteristics of low-altitude airspace render sensing an indispensable component of low-altitude wireless networks (LAWN). Although sensing techniques have been extensively studied under diverse paradigms, a prominent mismatch persists between state-of-the-art sensing schemes and the practical sensing demands of LAWN. To fill this research gap, this article systematically reviews LAWN-oriented sensing from the dimensions of system framework, core technologies, and research trends. Specifically, we first analyze the sensing system framework, covering concepts, services and tasks, nodes and targets, and scenarios for LAWN sensing. Next, we conduct a comparative analysis of existing sensing techniques from the perspectives of propagation medium, cooperation, methodology, and modality, analyzing their advantages and limitations. Then, we summarize promising future research directions for deployable LAWN sensing systems, covering non-cooperative and cooperative sensing, model-driven and data-driven sensing, and model-and-data-driven multi-modal sensing. Finally, we present a case study of a model-and-data-driven multi-modal method for real-time aerial target sensing. Compared with existing surveys on LAWN or sensing, this article delivers a more comprehensive, targeted review exclusively centered on LAWN sensing.

</details>


### [191] [Measuring the End-to-End Resilience of Application Deployments in Real-World Communication Networks with DRACO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10611)
*Leon Janzen,Matthias Hollick*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRACO框架，用于建模通信网络并测量应用部署的端到端弹性。通过在德国和法国的全国性应用部署案例，验证了该框架的有效性和数据兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数十亿用户依赖互联网连接访问全球应用，但通信网络作为关键基础设施，真实运营商很少披露其信息。缺乏拓扑和系统可用性数据，使得测量部署的端到端弹性变得困难。

Method: 提出DRACO框架，支持在任意通信网络上配置应用部署，并评估其可用性、鲁棒性和弹性。框架兼容多种公共数据源和合成数据。

Result: 通过建模德国和法国的全国性应用部署，展示了DRACO的功能范围，验证了其在多样化数据源下的兼容性与实用性。

Conclusion: DRACO为通信网络弹性评估提供了可配置、可扩展的建模工具，填补了关键基础设施数据稀缺带来的测量空白，有助于提升应用部署的可靠性分析能力。

Abstract: Billions of users take Internet connectivity for granted and use it daily to access applications deployed anywhere around the globe. What users perceive as a seamless connection between their end device and the application server involves a complex system of systems operated by various actors. Failures in any of these systems disrupt user-to-application connectivity if the communication network is not resilient. Since communication networks are a critical infrastructure as part of ICT, real-world operators rarely disclose information about them. This makes it difficult to measure the end-to-end resilience of deployments, because it requires guessing topologies and the availability of the involved systems.
  This paper presents DRACO, a framework for modeling communication networks and measuring end-to-end resilience of application deployments. It enables configuring application deployments across arbitrary communication networks and evaluating their availability, robustness, and resilience. We demonstrate the functional scope of DRACO by modeling nationwide application deployments in Germany and France, highlighting its compatibility with a wide variety of public data sources and synthetic data.

</details>


### [192] [Conversational Orchestration for Organic 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10714)
*Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型驱动的域代理的轻量级去中心化对话式编排框架，用于实现Organic 6G网络中跨域服务部署的简单操作、可扩展性和敏捷性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Organic 6G愿景需要跨边缘-云连续体和非地面资源的服务编排，要求简单操作、跨独立管理域的可扩展性以及在域动态加入/离开时的敏捷性。现有跨域编排方案依赖重型集成架构、多层协调器和深度遥测管道，导致部署困难且协调开销大。

Method: 采用基于大语言模型(LLM)驱动的域代理的去中心化编排框架。每个域保持自治，代理通过工具观察本地状态，闭环推理，并通过Agent-to-Agent (A2A)覆盖层与邻居代理交换摘要。通过周期性路由式可达性通告传播(延迟、瓶颈带宽、计算容量)实现快速可行部署，通过事件驱动请求和协商处理安全重优化、扩缩容和迁移。部署经基于验证器的自验证训练的紧凑推理模型，并通过影子更新进行周期性在线优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明：(1)随着域规模扩展和域加入时，控制平面开销可控且呈近线性增长；(2)决策质量稳健，包括目标变化后的恢复能力；(3)框架能够有效支持实时约束条件。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功实现了轻量级、去中心化的跨域编排，满足了Organic 6G网络对简单操作、可扩展性和敏捷性的需求。论文最后提出了有原则、安全且不确定性感知的代理编排未来研究方向。

Abstract: The Organic 6G vision of a network of networks spanning an edge-cloud continuum complemented by non-terrestrial resources requires, to realize its promise, service provisioning that is simple to operate, scalable across independently administered domains, and agile under domain churn (i.e., domains dynamically joining and leaving). Despite advances in cross-domain orchestration, many proposals rely on heavy integration fabrics, multi-layer coordinators, and deep telemetry pipelines that hinder deployability and amplify coordination overhead. We propose a lightweight, decentralized conversational orchestration framework based on Large Language Model (LLM)-driven domain agents. Each domain remains autonomous: an agent observes local state via tools, reasons in a closed loop, and exchanges summaries with neighboring agents over an Agent-to-Agent (A2A) overlay aligned with data-plane coupling. Fast feasible placement is enabled by periodic, routing-like dissemination of reachability advertisements (latency, bottleneck bandwidth, and compute capacity), while safe re-optimization, scaling, and migration are handled through event-driven requests and negotiation. To meet real-time constraints, we deploy a compact reasoning model trained with verifier-based self-verification and periodically refined online via shadow updates. Simulations show manageable, near-linear control-plane overhead as domains scale and during domain joins, and robust decision quality, including recovery after objective changes. We close by outlining future research directions for principled, secure, and uncertainty-aware agentic orchestration in Organic 6G.

</details>


### [193] [User Satisfaction-Aware Resource Allocation with Prospect-Theoretic Utility for Multimedia Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10625)
*Manoj Kumar S,Pranjal Varshney,Avhishek Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文基于前景理论提出了一种新的效用度量方法，用于捕获吞吐量变化对用户满意度的不对称影响，并设计了相应的资源分配策略，在多媒体流媒体中显著提升了用户满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多媒体流媒体资源分配策略主要关注吞吐量、公平性和延迟等指标，但忽略了吞吐量变化对用户满意度的不对称影响（即吞吐量下降造成的负面情绪远大于同等增长带来的满意度）。现有研究虽考虑吞吐量变化，却忽视了这一关键的行为经济学原理。

Method: 1) 基于前景理论提出一种通用效用度量方法，同时考虑平均吞吐量和吞吐量变化的不对称影响；2) 设计资源分配策略，在系统从资源充足转为资源稀缺状态时减少资源的急剧下降，同时尊重不同用户类别的优先级；3) 基于多队列系统理论分析提出快速数值方法确定最优阈值，以适应网络实例频繁变化的实际情况。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明，该资源分配策略在用户满意度方面优于现有的公平分配策略。研究还发现最优阈值的选择依赖于网络实例，而提出的快速数值方法能够在实际部署中有效确定最优阈值。

Conclusion: 本文成功将前景理论引入多媒体流媒体资源分配领域，提出的方法不仅能够更好地捕捉用户满意度，还提供了实用的快速计算方法，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional resource allocation policies for multimedia streaming have primarily targeted metrics such as throughput, fairness and delay. However, user satisfaction is known to be strongly influenced by variations in throughput. Although some recent works have considered throughput variations, they overlooked a well-known aspect of human behavior that has reshaped behavioral economics over the past few decades: the same amount of decrease in throughput causes greater annoyance than the satisfaction caused by the increase. Motivated by this insight from the broad field of economics, popularly known as prospect theory, we propose a generic utility (metric) that captures both the average throughput and the asymmetric effect of throughput variations on user satisfaction. Next, we propose a resource allocation policy that reduces abrupt decreases in allocated resources when the system transitions from a resource-rich to resource-scarce state while respecting the priorities of different classes of users. Extensive simulations show that this policy outperforms the existing fair allocation policies. However, the choice of the optimal threshold depends on the network instance, which, in practice, may change frequently. Hence, building on the theoretical analysis of multi-queue systems, we propose fast numerical methods for determining the optimal threshold, which can be used in field deployments.

</details>


### [194] [Media-over-Multipath-QUIC for Realtime Video Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10741)
*Tanya Shreedhar,Zuji Zhou,Nitinder Mohan,Fernando Kuipers*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoMQ扩展，通过利用MoQT中已有的媒体对象元数据，使传输调度器能够基于视频语义做出路径决策，显著降低实时视频的帧完成时延和播放缓冲需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多路径传输调度器仅能看到字节流，无法识别关键帧和增强帧的重要性差异，导致实时视频难以从多路径中获益。问题的关键不在于路径多样性不足，而在于缺乏一种机制让应用能够向传输层传递其无法看见的媒体语义信息。

Method: 基于已部署的Media over QUIC Transport (MoQT)协议，提出MoQT扩展。应用和中继操作者在边缘中继上安装声明式规则，这些规则匹配对象元数据并表达对标记路径的交付偏好。中继机械地评估规则，从而在包含无视频逻辑的情况下对视频语义进行操作。

Result: 在跨越Starlink和WiFi的实时测试平台上，四条规则将P99.9帧完成时间从最佳纯传输调度器下的384.7ms降低到114.1ms，并将所需播放缓冲减少61.5%。MoMQ是唯一满足150ms交互延迟目标的配置。在跨两国中继和跨大陆订阅者的部署中，相同规则无需修改即可应用并保持优势。

Conclusion: 通过将媒体对象元数据转化为路径决策，MoMQ成功地将应用层语义知识传递给传输调度器，在不增加复杂性的情况下显著改善了实时视频传输性能，证明了命名机制在多路径传输中的关键价值。

Abstract: Multipath transports place a client's WiFi, cellular, and satellite networks under one connection, yet real-time video gains little from them. The scheduler that assigns packets to paths sees only bytes, so it cannot tell a keyframe that anchors a second of video from an enhancement frame whose loss costs one image. We show that the limiting factor is not a shortage of path diversity but the absence of a channel through which the application can name what the transport cannot see. Media over QUIC Transport (MoQT), already deployed on production CDNs, carries media as named Objects, and relays forward each Object's metadata without interpreting it. The knowledge the scheduler lacks therefore already flows through the subscriber's relay. We present MoMQ, a MoQT extension that turns this metadata into path decisions. Applications and relay operators install declarative rules at the edge relay that match Object metadata and express delivery preferences against labeled paths. The relay evaluates the rules mechanically, so it acts on video semantics while containing no video logic. On a live testbed spanning Starlink and WiFi, four rules cut P99.9 frame completion time from 384.7 ms under the best transport-only scheduler to 114.1 ms and reduce the required playback buffer by 61.5%. MoMQ is the only configuration that meets the 150 ms interactive latency target. Across relays in two countries and subscribers on two continents, the same rules apply unchanged and retain their advantage wherever the paths remain disjoint.

</details>


### [195] [Energy-Efficient Joint Optimization of VLC and Fog Computing Resources Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10752)
*Wafaa B. M. Fadlelmula,Sanaa Hamid Mohamed,Taisir E. H. El-Gorashi,Jaafar M. H. Elmirghani*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种室内网络架构下的联合资源优化方法，通过联合优化可见光通信接入点选择、波长分配和雾计算资源分配，相比传统独立优化方法，实现了更低的平均总功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着室内无线通信需求的增长，如何降低网络能耗成为关键挑战。现有的接入点选择和波长分配方法通常基于最大化信干噪比而独立于雾计算资源位置，导致能耗较高。因此需要研究如何联合优化可见光通信和雾计算资源分配以降低处理和网络功耗。

Method: 提出一种混合整数线性规划(MILP)模型，联合优化三个关键要素：接入点选择、波长分配和雾计算资源分配。该模型以最小化处理和网络功耗为目标，同时满足用户请求的服务质量要求。

Result: 与基于最大化信干噪比的静态预配置接入点选择和波长分配方法相比，联合优化方法实现了更低的平均总功耗，主要驱动力来自于处理功耗的降低。这些能耗节省源于联合优化能够实现用户间的接入点共享，并选择仍能满足服务质量要求的更节能波长。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明，联合优化可见光通信和雾计算资源分配能够有效降低室内网络的总功耗，为实现绿色节能的室内网络架构提供了可行的技术方案，对未来智能建筑和绿色通信系统设计具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider an indoor networking architecture in which visible light communication (VLC) provides wireless connectivity between users and distributed fog computing resources and an energy-efficient passive optical network (PON)- based backhaul interconnects the VLC access points (APs).
