We investigate the joint optimization of the allocation of VLC and fog computing resources aiming to minimize processing and networking power consumption. We develop a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model that jointly optimizes access points (APs) selection, wavelength assignment and fog computing resources allocation to serve the requests of users. Compared to a statically preconfigured AP selection and wavelength assignment based on maximizing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) independently of fog resource placement, the joint optimization achieves lower average total power consumption, driven by a reduction in processing power. These savings are attributed to the ability of the joint optimization to allow AP sharing across users and selecting more energy-efficient wavelengths that still satisfy quality of service (QoS) requirements.

</details>


### [196] [Multi-UAV Tracking Evaluation Using 5G Uplink Signals on an O-RAN ISAC Simulation Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10784)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在O-RAN仿真测试平台上评估多目标检测、关联与跟踪的端到端性能，发现检测灵敏度高但跟踪性能受限于目标争用，高程信息对关联决策有重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对反无人机系统的指挥控制(C2)需求，传统仅关注检测性能的评估方式不足以反映实际应用需求，需要从端到端角度评估多目标检测、关联与跟踪的整体性能。

Method: 构建基于OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC和Sionna RT的O-RAN仿真测试平台，复用5G NR上行探测参考信号作为被动雷达波形，使用8元件平面接收阵列对3架不同高度、速度和雷达截面积的无人机进行双基配置测试，通过RIC xApp导出并发轨迹并与融合节点对接。

Result: 目标检测后跟踪性能受限：约74%的相干处理区间内两目标共享同一最近检测，检测频率远高于跟踪频率；阵列高程信息虽无法分离共享距离-多普勒单元的目标，但在58%的区间内决定关联；系统成功通过SAPIENT接口将轨迹导出至C2融合节点。

Conclusion: 多目标跟踪的关键瓶颈在于目标争用而非检测灵敏度，高程信息对目标关联决策具有重要作用，端到端系统评估揭示了影响多目标跟踪性能的关键因素。

Abstract: We evaluate multi-target detection, association and tracking end to end on an O-RAN simulation testbed built from OpenAirInterface, FlexRIC and Sionna RT that repurposes the 5G NR uplink sounding reference signal as a passive radar waveform, and against what a counter-UAS command-and- control (C2) consumer requires rather than by detection alone. Three UAVs differing in altitude, velocity and radar cross section (-8 to -20 dBsm) fly one bistatic pair with an 8-element planar receive array. Once every target is detected the binding limit is contention, not sensitivity - two targets share one nearest detection in about 74% of coherent processing intervals, and targets are detected far more often than they are tracked. Elevation from that array cannot separate targets sharing a range-Doppler cell, but it decides association in 58% of intervals. Concurrent tracks are exported from the RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC) xApp to a C2 fusion node over a SAPIENT interface carrying a calibrated detection confidence, validated at schema level against a mock fusion node. The evaluation is emulation-only on one geometry with 10 noise seeds, the mechanisms are characterized and analyzed to identify the key factors influencing multi-target tracking performance.

</details>


### [197] [A Systematic Sample Size Analysis of ML-Based Path Loss Prediction for LPWAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11083)
*Robert Bitterling,Christian Nettersheim,Jörn Hees,Michael Rademacher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究使用机器学习方法（随机森林和k-近邻算法）预测LoRa路径损耗，在城区部署场景下相比传统经验模型显著降低了预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRa等低功耗广域网络在智慧城市应用中日益普及，需要准确的路径损耗预测以进行有效的网络规划。然而传统的经验传播模型在这些场景中往往精度有限，因此需要更准确的预测方法。

Method: 研究使用随机森林结合激光雷达（LiDAR）提取的地形特征，以及k-近邻算法结合坐标数据，使用城区实际部署的真实测量数据。通过系统分析预测精度随训练集大小的变化，并与现有的经验模型和专用LPWAN模型进行对比。采用随机池化划分和留一网关验证两种评估方式。

Result: 在随机池化划分下，两种机器学习模型在所有评估的训练集规模上均优于基线模型。在最大训练集规模下，RMSE降至6.5 dB以下，而最佳基线模型为9.7 dB。留一网关验证显示随机森林模型对网关位置具有一定的泛化能力，但部分网关存在较大误差；仅使用坐标的k-NN在网关位置未知时性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法在LoRa路径损耗预测任务中表现出显著优势，能够实现准确的部署区域内插值，但在跨网关泛化时需要考虑模型选择和特征设计的影响。

Abstract: Low Power Wide Area Networks like LoRa are increasingly deployed for smart city applications, requiring accurate path loss prediction for effective network planning. Traditional (empirical) propagation models often exhibit limited accuracy in these scenarios. We investigate machine learning models for LoRa path loss prediction, systematically analyzing how prediction accuracy scales with training set size using real-world measurements from an urban deployment. Our approach employs a Random Forest with LiDAR-derived terrain features and k-Nearest Neighbors with coordinate data, comparing their performance against established empirical models and specialized LPWAN models. Under random pooled splits, both ML models consistently outperform the considered baseline models across the evaluated training-set sizes. At maximum training size, they achieve RMSE values below 6.5 dB compared to 9.7 dB for the best baseline, indicating accurate within-deployment interpolation. A leave-one-gateway-out check qualifies this result: RF shows placement-dependent transfer to held-out gateways, with moderate degradation for several gateways but larger errors for others, whereas coordinate-only k-NN degrades substantially when the gateway location is unseen

</details>
